# AI Audit Log

> **PA6 note:** File này là **phụ lục nhật ký** (prompt / output) các phiên dùng AI.  
> Bản khai báo chính thức về thư viện, framework, API, template, references và phạm vi AI-generated nằm ở:  
> **[`docs/Dependencies_And_AI_Disclosure.md`](./docs/Dependencies_And_AI_Disclosure.md)**  
> README hướng dẫn cài đặt/chạy/sử dụng: **[`README.md`](./README.md)**

## Mục đích

Ghi nhận quá trình nhóm sử dụng AI (Cursor / Claude) khi xây dựng tài liệu kỹ thuật và hỗ trợ mã nguồn SkillBridge MVP, phục vụ minh bạch khi bảo vệ đồ án.

## Tóm tắt nhanh

| Mục | Nội dung |
| :---- | :---- |
| Công cụ | Cursor Agent/Chat, Claude (qua Cursor) |
| Dùng cho | Docs kỹ thuật, scaffold/implement module, bugfix, README |
| Không dùng cho | Payment thật, gọi LLM runtime trong app |
| File disclosure chính | `docs/Dependencies_And_AI_Disclosure.md` |

---

Introduction\n\n### 1.1 Purpose\n\nThis document specifies the software requirements for the **Minimum Viable Product (MVP)** of the **SkillBridge** platform — a project-based marketplace that connects university students (Year 2–4, majoring in IT & Business/Economics) with small-and-medium enterprises (SMEs) in Ho Chi Minh City, Vietnam, through short-term, structured projects.\n\nThe MVP's primary goal is to **validate the core hypothesis**: *Are SMEs willing to post real projects, and are students willing to apply for and complete those projects through SkillBridge?*\n\n### 1.2 Scope\n\nThe MVP covers the **minimum end-to-end project lifecycle**:\n\n\`\`\`\nSME posts project → Student creates profile & applies → Semi-automatic matching\n→ Milestone tracking → SME acceptance → Verified Portfolio & Basic Certificate\n\`\`\`\n\nFeatures explicitly **out of scope** for the MVP (deferred to V1.1 / V2.0):\n\n- Integrated workspace (use Google Drive / GitHub / Discord as substitutes)\n- Real Escrow payment integration (MVP uses simulated escrow status)\n- Community Review System\n- Two-way rating system\n- Talent Pool for SMEs\n- Progress Log (detailed)\n- AI Matching\n- Mobile App\n- University API integration\n\n### 1.3 Definitions, Acronyms & Abbreviations\n\n| Term                  | Definition                                                                           |\n| :-------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **SME**               | Small and Medium Enterprise — the demand side of the platform                        |\n| **Student**           | University student (Year 2–4) — the supply side of the platform                     |\n| **Project**           | A short-term task (1–4 weeks) posted by an SME with a budget of ~1–5M VND           |\n| **Milestone**         | A defined checkpoint within a project with specific deliverables and deadlines        |\n| **Escrow (Simulated)**| A simulated payment holding mechanism (no real payment gateway in MVP)                |\n| **Matching**          | The process of connecting a student/group with a suitable project                     |\n| **Verified Portfolio**| A student's portfolio of completed projects, verified by the platform                 |\n| **Certificate**       | A digital completion certificate issued after successful project acceptance            |\n| **Acceptance**        | The process by which an SME reviews and approves the final deliverables               |\n| **MVP**               | Minimum Viable Product                                                                |\n| **ICP**               | Ideal Customer Profile                                                                |\n\n### 1.4 References\n\n- [Business Plan — SkillBridge (PA3)](./Business_Plan_Updated_PA3.md)\n- [System Architecture Document](./System_Architecture.md)\n- [Source Code Documentation](./Source_Code_Documentation.md)\n\n---\n\n## 2. Overall Description\n\n### 2.1 Product Perspective\n\nSkillBridge operates as a **two-sided project-based marketplace** positioned between:\n\n- **EdTech** (learning through real projects) and\n- **HR Tech** (flexible workforce sourcing).\n\nIt fills a market gap where:\n- Traditional job portals focus on full-time hiring (not micro-projects).\n- International freelance platforms require extensive experience.\n- Facebook/Zalo groups lack trust mechanisms (no escrow, no quality review).\n\n### 2.2 Product Functions (MVP Scope)\n\n| # | Function                               | MVP Status | Description                                                         |\n|---|----------------------------------------|:----------:|---------------------------------------------------------------------|\n| 1 | Project Posting by SME                 | ✅          | SMEs create structured project listings                             |\n| 2 | Student Skill Profile                  | ✅          | Students create profiles with skills, interests, and experience     |\n| 3 | Project Application (Apply)            | ✅          | Students browse and apply to projects (individual or groups of 2–4) |\n| 4 | Student Group Recommendation           | ✅          | Semi-automatic suggestion of matching student groups                |\n| 5 | Milestone Tracking                     | ✅          | Basic progress tracking with defined milestones                     |\n| 6 | Escrow Payment (Simulated)             | ✅          | Status simulation of deposit/disbursement (no real gateway)         |\n| 7 | Acceptance Mechanism                   | ✅          | SME reviews and approves final deliverables                         |\n| 8 | Verified Portfolio                     | ✅          | Auto-update student portfolio after acceptance                      |\n| 9 | Completion Certificate                 | ✅          | Issue basic digital certificate after acceptance                    |\n| 10| External Collaboration (Workaround)    | ✅          | Links to Google Drive / GitHub / Discord for collaboration          |\n\n### 2.3 User Characteristics\n\n| User Type     | Characteristics                                                                 |\n| :------------ | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |\n| **Student**   | Year 2–4, IT/Business major, tech-savvy, primarily uses mobile & web            |\n| **SME**       | Small business owner/founder, limited tech expertise, budget-conscious           |\n| **Admin**     | Platform operator (founding team), manages matching, moderation, and operations  |\n\n### 2.4 Operating Environment\n\n- **Platform**: Web application (responsive for mobile browsers)\n- **Frontend**: Modern SPA framework (React / Next.js)\n- **Backend**: Node.js / Express with REST API\n- **Database**: PostgreSQL (relational)\n- **Hosting**: Cloud-based (AWS / Vercel / Railway)\n- **Authentication**: JWT-based with email/password + optional social login (Google)\n\n### 2.5 Design Constraints\n\n- The MVP must be developed and deployed within **Q3/2026** (July–September).\n- The team consists of **2 developers** (CTO + CEO with engineering background).\n- Budget for infrastructure is **minimal** — prefer free-tier cloud services where possible.\n- No real payment gateway integration in MVP; escrow is simulated via status flags.\n\n---\n\n## 3. User Roles & Personas\n\n### 3.1 Student (Supply Side)\n\n- **Registration**: Create an account with email, university name, major, year.\n- **Profile**: Fill in skills, interests, past projects, and relevant experience.\n- **Browse Projects**: View available projects filtered by skill, category, or budget.\n- **Apply**: Apply to a project individually or form a group of 2–4 students.\n- **Execute Project**: Work on assigned milestones; update status on the platform.\n- **Receive Certificate**: Get a verified portfolio entry and certificate upon acceptance.\n\n### 3.2 SME (Demand Side)\n\n- **Registration**: Create an account with company name, industry, contact info.\n- **Post Project**: Define project scope, requirements, skills needed, budget, and timeline.\n- **Review Applications**: Browse student profiles and applications.\n- **Select Student/Group**: Choose the best fit for the project.\n- **Track Progress**: Monitor milestone completion.\n- **Accept Deliverables**: Review and approve the final output, triggering escrow disbursement and certificate issuance.\n\n### 3.3 Admin (Platform Operator)\n\n- **Moderate Content**: Review and approve project postings.\n- **Facilitate Matching**: Manually assist in matching students to projects where the semi-automatic recommendation needs human judgment.\n- **Manage Users**: Handle account issues, disputes, and support requests.\n- **Monitor Operations**: Track KPIs (projects posted, applications, matching rate, completion rate).\n\n---\n\n## 4. Functional Requirements\n\n### 4.1 Authentication & Authorization\n\n| ID       | Requirement                                                                          | Priority |\n| :------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-AUTH-01 | The system SHALL allow users to register as either **Student** or **SME**.          | Must     |\n| FR-AUTH-02 | The system SHALL authenticate users via email/password.                              | Must     |\n| FR-AUTH-03 | The system SHOULD support Google OAuth for quick registration/login.                | Should   |\n| FR-AUTH-04 | The system SHALL issue JWT tokens for session management.                            | Must     |\n| FR-AUTH-05 | The system SHALL enforce role-based access control (Student, SME, Admin).            | Must     |\n| FR-AUTH-06 | The system SHALL allow users to reset their password via email.                      | Must     |\n\n### 4.2 Student Profile Management\n\n| ID         | Requirement                                                                        | Priority |\n| :--------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-PROF-01 | Students SHALL be able to create and edit their profile.                            | Must     |\n| FR-PROF-02 | Profile SHALL include: full name, university, major, year, bio, skills (tags), links to personal projects/GitHub. | Must |\n| FR-PROF-03 | Profile SHALL display the student's Verified Portfolio (list of completed projects). | Must    |\n| FR-PROF-04 | Profile SHOULD display skill-level indicators (beginner/intermediate/advanced).     | Should   |\n| FR-PROF-05 | Profile SHALL be viewable by SMEs when reviewing applications.                      | Must     |\n\n### 4.3 Project Posting (SME)\n\n| ID         | Requirement                                                                        | Priority |\n| :--------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-PROJ-01 | SMEs SHALL be able to create a new project posting.                                | Must     |\n| FR-PROJ-02 | Project posting SHALL include: title, description, required skills (tags), budget (VND), duration, number of milestones, expected deliverables. | Must |\n| FR-PROJ-03 | Project posting SHALL include a list of milestones with descriptions and deadlines. | Must     |\n| FR-PROJ-04 | Project posting SHALL go through Admin review before being published.               | Must     |\n| FR-PROJ-05 | SMEs SHALL be able to edit/update a project before it is matched.                   | Must     |\n| FR-PROJ-06 | SMEs SHALL be able to close/cancel a project that has not yet been matched.         | Must     |\n| FR-PROJ-07 | The system SHALL categorize projects by skill area (e.g., Web Dev, Data Analysis, Marketing, Content). | Should |\n\n### 4.4 Project Application (Student)\n\n| ID         | Requirement                                                                        | Priority |\n| :--------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-APPLY-01 | Students SHALL be able to browse a list of published projects.                     | Must     |\n| FR-APPLY-02 | Students SHALL be able to filter/search projects by skill, category, budget, and duration. | Must |\n| FR-APPLY-03 | Students SHALL be able to apply to a project individually.                         | Must     |\n| FR-APPLY-04 | Students SHALL be able to apply to a project as a group (2–4 members).             | Must     |\n| FR-APPLY-05 | The application SHALL include a cover message explaining the student's interest and relevant experience. | Should |\n| FR-APPLY-06 | Students SHALL be able to withdraw an application before matching is confirmed.    | Must     |\n\n### 4.5 Matching\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                                       | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-MATCH-01 | The system SHALL recommend suitable students/groups based on skill-tag overlap.    | Must     |\n| FR-MATCH-02 | SMEs SHALL be able to review recommended students/groups and their profiles.       | Must     |\n| FR-MATCH-03 | SMEs SHALL be able to select and confirm a student/group for the project.          | Must     |\n| FR-MATCH-04 | The system SHALL notify the selected student/group upon confirmation.              | Must     |\n| FR-MATCH-05 | The student/group SHALL confirm acceptance of the match before the project begins. | Must    |\n| FR-MATCH-06 | Admin SHALL have the ability to manually intervene in the matching process.        | Must     |\n\n### 4.6 Milestone Tracking\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                                       | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-MILE-01  | Each project SHALL have a list of predefined milestones.                           | Must     |\n| FR-MILE-02  | Each milestone SHALL have: title, description, deadline, status (Not Started / In Progress / Submitted / Approved). | Must |\n| FR-MILE-03  | Students SHALL be able to submit a milestone for review.                           | Must     |\n| FR-MILE-04  | SMEs SHALL be able to review and approve/reject a submitted milestone.             | Must     |\n| FR-MILE-05  | The system SHALL display an overview of all milestones and their statuses for both parties. | Must |\n| FR-MILE-06  | The system SHOULD send notifications when a milestone is submitted or reviewed.    | Should   |\n\n### 4.7 Escrow Payment (Simulated)\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                                       | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-ESC-01   | The system SHALL simulate an escrow status for each project (Pending → Deposited → Disbursed). | Must |\n| FR-ESC-02   | When a match is confirmed, the escrow status SHALL change to \"Deposited\" (simulated). | Must   |\n| FR-ESC-03   | When the project is fully accepted, the escrow status SHALL change to \"Disbursed\" (simulated). | Must |\n| FR-ESC-04   | The system SHALL display the current escrow status to both SME and Student.        | Must     |\n| FR-ESC-05   | No real money transaction SHALL occur in the MVP.                                  | Must     |\n\n### 4.8 Acceptance Mechanism\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                                       | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-ACPT-01  | SMEs SHALL be able to review the final deliverables of a project.                  | Must     |\n| FR-ACPT-02  | SMEs SHALL be able to approve (accept) or request revisions for the project.       | Must     |\n| FR-ACPT-03  | Upon acceptance, the system SHALL trigger: escrow disbursement (simulated), portfolio update, certificate generation. | Must |\n| FR-ACPT-04  | The system SHALL mark the project as \"Completed\" after acceptance.                 | Must     |\n\n### 4.9 Verified Portfolio & Certificate\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                                       | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-PORT-01  | Upon project acceptance, the system SHALL automatically add the project to the student's Verified Portfolio. | Must |\n| FR-PORT-02  | The portfolio entry SHALL include: project title, description, role, deliverables, SME confirmation status, completion date. | Must |\n| FR-PORT-03  | The system SHALL generate a basic digital certificate (PDF or in-app display) for the student. | Must |\n| FR-PORT-04  | The certificate SHALL include: student name, project title, SME name, completion date, and platform verification badge. | Must |\n| FR-PORT-05  | Students SHALL be able to view and share their Verified Portfolio publicly.        | Must     |\n\n### 4.10 Notifications\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                                       | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-NOTIF-01 | The system SHOULD send email notifications for key events (new application, match confirmed, milestone submitted, project accepted). | Should |\n| FR-NOTIF-02 | The system SHOULD display in-app notifications for the same events.               | Should   |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Non-Functional Requirements\n\n### 5.1 Performance\n\n| ID         | Requirement                                                                        | Priority |\n| :--------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| NFR-PERF-01 | Page load time SHALL be ≤ 3 seconds for primary pages (project list, profile, dashboard). | Must |\n| NFR-PERF-02 | API response time SHALL be ≤ 500ms for 95% of requests under normal load.         | Must     |\n| NFR-PERF-03 | The system SHALL support at least **100 concurrent users** during the Pilot phase. | Must     |\n\n### 5.2 Security\n\n| ID         | Requirement                                                                        | Priority |\n| :--------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| NFR-SEC-01 | All passwords SHALL be hashed using bcrypt (or equivalent) before storage.         | Must     |\n| NFR-SEC-02 | All API endpoints SHALL enforce authentication and authorization.                   | Must     |\n| NFR-SEC-03 | The system SHALL use HTTPS for all communications.                                  | Must     |\n| NFR-SEC-04 | JWT tokens SHALL have a configurable expiration time (default: 24 hours).           | Must     |\n| NFR-SEC-05 | The system SHALL protect against common vulnerabilities (XSS, CSRF, SQL Injection). | Must    |\n\n### 5.3 Scalability\n\n| ID         | Requirement                                                                        | Priority |\n| :--------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| NFR-SCAL-01 | The architecture SHALL be designed to scale horizontally (add more server instances). | Should |\n| NFR-SCAL-02 | Database design SHALL avoid tight coupling to enable future microservice decomposition. | Should |\n\n### 5.4 Usability\n\n| ID         | Requirement                                                                        | Priority |\n| :--------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| NFR-USE-01 | The UI SHALL be responsive and usable on both desktop and mobile browsers.         | Must     |\n| NFR-USE-02 | Key user flows SHALL be completable in ≤ 5 steps (e.g., posting a project, applying). | Should |\n| NFR-USE-03 | The system SHALL provide clear error messages and form validation feedback.        | Must     |\n| NFR-USE-04 | The interface SHALL use Vietnamese as the primary language, with English as secondary. | Must  |\n\n### 5.5 Reliability & Availability\n\n| ID         | Requirement                                                                        | Priority |\n| :--------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| NFR-REL-01 | The system SHALL target **99% uptime** during the Pilot period.                    | Must     |\n| NFR-REL-02 | The system SHALL implement automated database backups (daily).                     | Must     |\n\n### 5.6 Maintainability\n\n| ID         | Requirement                                                                        | Priority |\n| :--------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| NFR-MAIN-01 | The codebase SHALL follow established coding conventions (see [Source Code Documentation](./Source_Code_Documentation.md)). | Must |\n| NFR-MAIN-02 | The codebase SHALL include unit tests for critical business logic (≥ 60% coverage for core modules). | Should |\n| NFR-MAIN-03 | The system SHALL use structured logging for debugging and monitoring.              | Should   |\n\n---\n\n## 6. Use Cases\n\n### 6.1 UC-01: SME Posts a New Project\n\n| Field             | Description                                                                   |\n| :---------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Actor**         | SME                                                                           |\n| **Precondition**  | SME is registered and logged in                                               |\n| **Main Flow**     | 1. SME navigates to \"Post Project\" page<br>2. SME fills in project details (title, description, skills, budget, duration, milestones)<br>3. SME submits the project<br>4. Admin reviews and approves the project<br>5. Project is published on the platform |\n| **Postcondition** | Project is visible to students for browsing and applying                       |\n| **Alternate Flow**| 4a. Admin rejects the project → SME is notified with reason → SME can edit and resubmit |\n\n### 6.2 UC-02: Student Creates Profile & Applies to a Project\n\n| Field             | Description                                                                   |\n| :---------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Actor**         | Student                                                                       |\n| **Precondition**  | Student is registered and logged in; profile is complete                       |\n| **Main Flow**     | 1. Student browses project listings<br>2. Student selects a project<br>3. Student applies with an optional cover message<br>4. Application is recorded and visible to the SME |\n| **Postcondition** | Application appears in the SME's review queue                                  |\n| **Alternate Flow**| 3a. Student can apply as a group → invites other students to form a group → all members are linked to the application |\n\n### 6.3 UC-03: Matching — SME Selects a Student/Group\n\n| Field             | Description                                                                   |\n| :---------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Actor**         | SME, Admin                                                                    |\n| **Precondition**  | At least one student/group has applied to the project                          |\n| **Main Flow**     | 1. SME reviews applications and recommended student profiles<br>2. SME selects a student/group<br>3. System notifies the selected student/group<br>4. Student/group confirms the match<br>5. Project status changes to \"In Progress\"; escrow status changes to \"Deposited\" |\n| **Postcondition** | The project is matched and ready for execution                                 |\n| **Alternate Flow**| 4a. Student/group declines → SME is notified → SME selects another applicant   |\n\n### 6.4 UC-04: Milestone Tracking & Submission\n\n| Field             | Description                                                                   |\n| :---------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Actor**         | Student, SME                                                                  |\n| **Precondition**  | Project is matched and in progress                                             |\n| **Main Flow**     | 1. Student works on the current milestone<br>2. Student submits the milestone (marks as \"Submitted\")<br>3. SME reviews the milestone deliverables<br>4. SME approves the milestone (marks as \"Approved\") |\n| **Postcondition** | Milestone is marked as completed; next milestone becomes active                |\n| **Alternate Flow**| 4a. SME requests revisions → Student re-works and resubmits                    |\n\n### 6.5 UC-05: Project Acceptance & Certificate Issuance\n\n| Field             | Description                                                                   |\n| :---------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Actor**         | SME, System                                                                   |\n| **Precondition**  | All milestones are approved                                                    |\n| **Main Flow**     | 1. SME reviews the final deliverables<br>2. SME approves the project<br>3. System changes escrow status to \"Disbursed\"<br>4. System adds the project to the student's Verified Portfolio<br>5. System generates a completion certificate |\n| **Postcondition** | Project is marked as \"Completed\"; student has updated portfolio and certificate |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Data Models\n\n### 7.1 Entity-Relationship Overview\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\nerDiagram\n    USER ||--o| STUDENT_PROFILE : has\n    USER ||--o| SME_PROFILE : has\n    USER {\n        uuid id PK\n        string email\n        string password_hash\n        enum role \"STUDENT | SME | ADMIN\"\n        datetime created_at\n        datetime updated_at\n    }\n\n    STUDENT_PROFILE {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid user_id FK\n        string full_name\n        string university\n        string major\n        int year\n        text bio\n        json skills \"array of skill tags\"\n        string github_url\n        string portfolio_url\n    }\n\n    SME_PROFILE {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid user_id FK\n        string company_name\n        string industry\n        text description\n        string contact_phone\n        string website\n    }\n\n    PROJECT {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid sme_id FK\n        string title\n        text description\n        json required_skills \"array of skill tags\"\n        decimal budget\n        int duration_weeks\n        enum status \"DRAFT | PENDING_REVIEW | PUBLISHED | MATCHED | IN_PROGRESS | COMPLETED | CANCELLED\"\n        enum escrow_status \"NONE | DEPOSITED | DISBURSED\"\n        datetime created_at\n        datetime updated_at\n    }\n\n    MILESTONE {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        string title\n        text description\n        datetime deadline\n        int order_index\n        enum status \"NOT_STARTED | IN_PROGRESS | SUBMITTED | APPROVED | REVISION_REQUESTED\"\n    }\n\n    APPLICATION {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        uuid student_id FK\n        uuid group_id FK \"nullable\"\n        text cover_message\n        enum status \"PENDING | ACCEPTED | REJECTED | WITHDRAWN\"\n        datetime created_at\n    }\n\n    STUDENT_GROUP {\n        uuid id PK\n        string name\n        uuid leader_id FK\n        datetime created_at\n    }\n\n    STUDENT_GROUP_MEMBER {\n        uuid group_id FK\n        uuid student_id FK\n    }\n\n    VERIFIED_PORTFOLIO_ENTRY {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid student_id FK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        string project_title\n        text project_description\n        string role\n        text deliverables\n        datetime completed_at\n    }\n\n    CERTIFICATE {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid student_id FK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        string student_name\n        string project_title\n        string sme_name\n        datetime issued_at\n        string verification_code\n    }\n\n    SME_PROFILE ||--o{ PROJECT : posts\n    PROJECT ||--o{ MILESTONE : contains\n    PROJECT ||--o{ APPLICATION : receives\n    STUDENT_PROFILE ||--o{ APPLICATION : submits\n    STUDENT_GROUP ||--o{ STUDENT_GROUP_MEMBER : has\n    STUDENT_PROFILE ||--o{ STUDENT_GROUP_MEMBER : belongs_to\n    STUDENT_PROFILE ||--o{ VERIFIED_PORTFOLIO_ENTRY : earns\n    STUDENT_PROFILE ||--o{ CERTIFICATE : receives\n    PROJECT ||--o| VERIFIED_PORTFOLIO_ENTRY : generates\n    PROJECT ||--o| CERTIFICATE : generates\n\`\`\`\n\n### 7.2 Key Enumerations\n\n| Enum Name        | Values                                                                     |\n| :--------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `UserRole`       | `STUDENT`, `SME`, `ADMIN`                                                  |\n| `ProjectStatus`  | `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `PUBLISHED`, `MATCHED`, `IN_PROGRESS`, `COMPLETED`, `CANCELLED` |\n| `EscrowStatus`   | `NONE`, `DEPOSITED`, `DISBURSED`                                           |\n| `MilestoneStatus`| `NOT_STARTED`, `IN_PROGRESS`, `SUBMITTED`, `APPROVED`, `REVISION_REQUESTED`|\n| `ApplicationStatus` | `PENDING`, `ACCEPTED`, `REJECTED`, `WITHDRAWN`                         |\n\n---\n\n## 8. External Interfaces\n\n### 8.1 User Interface\n\n- **Web Application** (SPA) — responsive design supporting desktop and mobile browsers.\n- Primary language: **Vietnamese** with English fallback for technical terms.\n- Key screens:\n  - Home / Landing Page\n  - Registration / Login\n  - Student Dashboard (projects, applications, portfolio)\n  - SME Dashboard (posted projects, applications, milestone review)\n  - Project Listing / Search\n  - Project Detail / Apply\n  - Milestone Tracking View\n  - Profile Page (Student / SME)\n  - Admin Panel (moderation, matching, KPIs)\n\n### 8.2 External Services\n\n| Service              | Purpose                                      | MVP Integration |\n| :------------------- | :------------------------------------------- | :-------------: |\n| Email Service (SMTP) | Send notification emails                     | Should          |\n| Google OAuth         | Social login                                 | Should          |\n| Cloud Storage        | Store uploaded files (profile pictures, etc.) | Should          |\n| PDF Generation       | Generate completion certificates             | Must            |\n\n### 8.3 Collaboration Tools (External — MVP Workaround)\n\n| Tool          | Purpose                            |\n| :------------ | :--------------------------------- |\n| Google Drive  | File sharing between SME & Student |\n| GitHub        | Source code collaboration          |\n| Discord       | Real-time communication            |\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> These external tools are temporary workarounds for the MVP. Integrated workspace features will be developed in V1.1.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Constraints & Assumptions\n\n### 9.1 Constraints\n\n1. **Budget**: Minimal infrastructure budget; prefer free-tier services.\n2. **Team Size**: 2 full-time developers (CTO + CEO).\n3. **Timeline**: MVP must be deployable by end of **September 2026** (Q3).\n4. **No Real Payments**: Escrow is simulated; no payment gateway integration.\n5. **Pilot Scale**: Target ≤ 25 students, ≤ 8 SMEs, ≤ 10 projects in 8 weeks.\n\n### 9.2 Assumptions\n\n1. Students have reliable internet access and modern web browsers.\n2. SMEs can articulate their project requirements in a structured form.\n3. Admin (founding team) is available to manually support matching and moderation during Pilot.\n4. External collaboration tools (Google Drive, GitHub, Discord) are familiar to both user groups.\n5. Email delivery via SMTP is sufficient for MVP notifications.\n\n### 9.3 Risks\n\n| Risk                                         | Impact | Mitigation                                                  |\n| :------------------------------------------- | :----: | :---------------------------------------------------------- |\n| Low SME engagement (no projects posted)       | High   | Seed projects from personal network; free pilot participation |\n| Low student engagement                        | High   | Pre-launch community building; mock projects for onboarding  |\n| Quality issues in student deliverables        | Medium | Structured milestones; admin support; SME review at each step |\n| Technical debt from fast MVP development      | Medium | Enforce coding conventions; plan for refactoring in V1.1      |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Appendices\n\n### 10.1 MVP Success Criteria (Go/No-Go for V1.1)\n\n| KPI                                        | Target                               |\n| :----------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------- |\n| Number of projects posted by SMEs          | ≥ 10 in 8-week pilot                 |\n| Application rate (applications / project)  | ≥ 3 applicants per project           |\n| Matching success rate                      | ≥ 60% of projects matched            |\n| Project completion rate                    | ≥ 50% of matched projects accepted   |\n| Qualitative feedback                       | ≥ 70% of pilot users willing to reuse |\n\n### 10.2 Pilot Scope\n\n| Metric          | Target        |\n| :-------------- | :------------ |\n| Students        | 15–25         |\n| SMEs            | 5–8           |\n| Projects        | ≥ 10          |\n| Duration        | 8 weeks       |\n| Geography       | Ho Chi Minh City |\n\n### 10.3 Feature Evolution Matrix\n\n| Feature                        | MVP              | V1.1 (Q4/2026)         | V2.0 (Q1/2027)         |\n| :----------------------------- | :--------------- | :---------------------- | :---------------------- |\n| Project Posting (SME)          | ✅               | ✅                      | ✅                      |\n| Student Profile & Apply        | ✅               | ✅ Progress Log       | ✅                      |\n| Escrow Payment                 | Simulated        | Real integration        | ✅                      |\n| Acceptance                     | ✅               | ✅                      | ✅                      |\n| Verified Portfolio & Cert      | Basic            | ✅                      | ✅ + Paid QR cert       |\n| Community Review               | — [Introduction](#1-introduction)\n2. [Architectural Goals & Constraints](#2-architectural-goals--constraints)\n3. [High-Level Architecture](#3-high-level-architecture)\n4. [Technology Stack](#4-technology-stack)\n5. [Frontend Architecture](#5-frontend-architecture)\n6. [Backend Architecture](#6-backend-architecture)\n7. [Database Design](#7-database-design)\n8. [Security Architecture](#11-security-architecture)\n12. [Performance & Scalability](#12-performance--scalability)\n13. [Monitoring & Logging](#13-monitoring--logging)\n14. [Evolution Roadmap](#14-evolution-roadmap)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Introduction\n\n### 1.1 Purpose\n\nThis document describes the system architecture for the **SkillBridge MVP** — a two-sided project-based marketplace connecting university students with SMEs through short-term, structured projects. The architecture is designed to support the MVP's core lifecycle:\n\n\`\`\`\nSME posts project → Student creates profile & applies → Semi-automatic matching\n→ Milestone tracking → SME acceptance → Verified Portfolio & Certificate\n\`\`\`\n\n### 1.2 Scope\n\nThis architecture covers:\n- Frontend web application (SPA)\n- Backend REST API server\n- Database layer\n- Authentication system\n- Deployment infrastructure\n- Security measures\n\n### 1.3 References\n\n- [Software Requirements Specification (SRS)](./SRS_MVP.md)\n- [Source Code Documentation](./Source_Code_Documentation.md)\n- [Business Plan — SkillBridge (PA3)](./Business_Plan_Updated_PA3.md)\n\n---\n\n## 2. Architectural Goals & Constraints\n\n### 2.1 Goals\n\n| Goal                    | Description                                                                |\n| :---------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Rapid Development**   | MVP must be deployable within Q3/2026 by a 2-person dev team               |\n| **Simplicity**          | Monolithic architecture suitable for a small team; avoid over-engineering   |\n| **Extensibility**       | Design for future modularization (V1.1: escrow integration, V2.0: AI)      |\n| **Cost Efficiency**     | Leverage free-tier cloud services; minimal operational overhead             |\n| **Developer Experience**| Use modern, well-supported frameworks with strong ecosystems               |\n\n### 2.2 Constraints\n\n| Constraint               | Impact                                                                   |\n| :------------------------ | :----------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| 2-person dev team         | Must use familiar technologies; avoid complex distributed systems        |\n| Minimal budget            | Free-tier cloud hosting; no paid third-party APIs in MVP                 |\n| 8-week pilot window       | Architecture must support rapid iteration and deployment                  |\n| No real payment gateway   | Escrow is simulated via status flags only                                 |\n| Pilot scale: ~50 users    | No need for high-availability or complex scaling in MVP                   |\n\n### 2.3 Architectural Principles\n\n1. **Modular Monolith**: Start as a well-structured monolith, organized by feature/domain modules, enabling future decomposition.\n2. **API-First**: Frontend and backend communicate exclusively via REST APIs, allowing independent evolution.\n3. **Convention over Configuration**: Follow established patterns and conventions to reduce decision fatigue.\n4. **Separation of Concerns**: Clear boundaries between presentation, business logic, and data access layers.\n5. **Security by Design**: Authentication, authorization, and input validation built in from the start.\n\n---\n\n## 3. High-Level Architecture\n\n### 3.1 System Context\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"External Users\"\n        Student[\"🎓 Student\"]\n        SME[\"🏢 SME\"]\n        Admin[\"⚙️ Admin\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph \"SkillBridge Platform\"\n        WebApp[\"Web Application<br>(React / Next.js)\"]\n        APIServer[\"API Server<br>(Node.js / Express)\"]\n        DB[\"Database<br>(PostgreSQL)\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph \"External Services\"\n        Email[\"Email Service<br>(SMTP / SendGrid)\"]\n        OAuth[\"Google OAuth\"]\n        Storage[\"Cloud Storage<br>(S3 / Cloudinary)\"]\n    end\n\n    Student -->|HTTPS| WebApp\n    SME -->|HTTPS| WebApp\n    Admin -->|HTTPS| WebApp\n\n    WebApp -->|REST API| APIServer\n    APIServer -->|SQL| DB\n    APIServer -->|SMTP| Email\n    APIServer -->|OAuth 2.0| OAuth\n    APIServer -->|Upload/Download| Storage\n\`\`\`\n\n### 3.2 Component Architecture\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\ngraph LR\n    subgraph \"Frontend - SPA\"\n        Pages[\"Pages / Routes\"]\n        Components[\"UI Components\"]\n        State[\"State Management\"]\n        APIClient[\"API Client (Axios/Fetch)\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph \"Backend - API Server\"\n        Router[\"Express Router\"]\n        Middleware[\"Middleware Stack\"]\n        Controllers[\"Controllers\"]\n        Services[\"Business Logic Services\"]\n        DAL[\"Data Access Layer\"]\n        Utils[\"Utilities\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph \"Data Layer\"\n        PG[\"PostgreSQL\"]\n    end\n\n    Pages --> Components\n    Pages --> State\n    State --> APIClient\n    APIClient -->|HTTP/JSON| Router\n\n    Router --> Middleware\n    Middleware --> Controllers\n    Controllers --> Services\n    Services --> DAL\n    DAL --> PG\n\`\`\`\n\n---\n\n## 4. Technology Stack\n\n### 4.1 Overview\n\n| Layer                | Technology                  | Rationale                                                    |\n| :------------------- | :-------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Frontend**         | Next.js (React)             | SSR/SSG support, file-based routing, excellent DX, large ecosystem |\n| **Styling**          | CSS Modules / Vanilla CSS   | No external dependencies; scoped styles; full flexibility     |\n| **Backend**          | Node.js + Express           | Lightweight, fast I/O, JavaScript full-stack synergy          |\n| **Database**         | PostgreSQL                  | Reliable, ACID-compliant, excellent JSON support, free-tier available |\n| **ORM**              | Prisma                      | Type-safe queries, auto-migration, excellent DX               |\n| **Authentication**   | JWT + bcrypt                | Stateless auth, industry standard, simple to implement        |\n| **API Protocol**     | REST (JSON)                 | Simple, well-understood, sufficient for MVP                   |\n| **Email**            | Nodemailer (SMTP)           | Simple email sending, free with Gmail SMTP for MVP            |\n| **File Storage**     | Cloudinary / Local          | Free-tier image hosting; can migrate to S3 later              |\n| **Version Control**  | Git + GitHub                | Standard, free, team collaboration built-in                   |\n| **CI/CD**            | GitHub Actions              | Free for public repos, integrated with GitHub                 |\n| **Hosting**          | Vercel (FE) + Railway (BE)  | Free-tier suitable for MVP; auto-deploy from GitHub           |\n\n### 4.2 Version Matrix\n\n| Package           | Version    |\n| :---------------- | :--------- |\n| Node.js           | ≥ 20 LTS   |\n| Next.js           | ≥ 14       |\n| React             | ≥ 18       |\n| Express           | ≥ 4.18     |\n| PostgreSQL        | ≥ 15       |\n| Prisma            | ≥ 5        |\n| TypeScript        | ≥ 5        |\n\n> [!IMPORTANT]\n> The entire stack uses **TypeScript** for both frontend and backend to ensure type safety and reduce runtime errors.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Frontend Architecture\n\n### 5.1 Project Structure\n\n\`\`\`\nfrontend/\n├── public/                     # Static assets (images, fonts, favicon)\n├── src/\n│   ├── app/                    # Next.js App Router (pages & layouts)\n│   │   ├── (auth)/             # Auth-related pages (login, register)\n│   │   ├── (dashboard)/        # Dashboard pages (student, SME, admin)\n│   │   ├── projects/           # Project listing & detail pages\n│   │   ├── profile/            # Profile pages\n│   │   ├── layout.tsx          # Root layout\n│   │   └── page.tsx            # Home page\n│   ├── components/             # Reusable UI components\n│   │   ├── common/             # Buttons, inputs, modals, cards\n│   │   ├── layout/             # Header, footer, sidebar, navigation\n│   │   ├── project/            # Project-specific components\n│   │   ├── profile/            # Profile-specific components\n│   │   └── milestone/          # Milestone-specific components\n│   ├── hooks/                  # Custom React hooks\n│   ├── lib/                    # Utility functions & API client\n│   │   ├── api.ts              # Axios/Fetch API client configuration\n│   │   ├── auth.ts             # Auth helpers (token management)\n│   │   └── utils.ts            # General utilities\n│   ├── types/                  # TypeScript type definitions\n│   ├── styles/                 # Global styles & CSS modules\n│   │   ├── globals.css         # Global styles & design tokens\n│   │   └── variables.css       # CSS custom properties\n│   └── context/                # React Context providers (auth, theme)\n├── next.config.js\n├── tsconfig.json\n├── package.json\n└── .env.local                  # Environment variables\n\`\`\`\n\n### 5.2 Key Design Patterns\n\n- **App Router (Next.js 14+)**: File-based routing with layouts and loading states.\n- **Component Composition**: Small, focused components composed into page-level views.\n- **Custom Hooks**: Encapsulate API calls, form logic, and state management.\n- **Context API**: Global state for auth (user session) and theme preferences.\n- **CSS Modules**: Scoped styling to avoid class name collisions.\n\n### 5.3 State Management Strategy\n\n| State Type         | Approach                           | Example                           |\n| :----------------- | :--------------------------------- | :-------------------------------- |\n| **Server State**   | React Query / SWR                  | Project list, applications        |\n| **Auth State**     | React Context + localStorage       | Current user, JWT token           |\n| **UI State**       | Local component state (`useState`) | Modal open/close, form inputs     |\n| **Form State**     | React Hook Form                    | Project posting, profile editing  |\n\n### 5.4 API Client\n\n\`\`\`typescript\n// src/lib/api.ts — Simplified example\nimport axios from 'axios';\n\nconst api = axios.create({\n  baseURL: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL,\n  headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n});\n\n// Request interceptor — attach JWT token\napi.interceptors.request.use((config) => {\n  const token = localStorage.getItem('token');\n  if (token) {\n    config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;\n  }\n  return config;\n});\n\n// Response interceptor — handle 401 (redirect to login)\napi.interceptors.response.use(\n  (response) => response,\n  (error) => {\n    if (error.response?.status === 401) {\n      localStorage.removeItem('token');\n      window.location.href = '/login';\n    }\n    return Promise.reject(error);\n  }\n);\n\nexport default api;\n\`\`\`\n\n---\n\n## 6. Backend Architecture\n\n### 6.1 Project Structure\n\n\`\`\`\nbackend/\n├── src/\n│   ├── config/                 # Configuration files\n│   │   ├── database.ts         # Database connection config\n│   │   ├── env.ts              # Environment variable validation\n│   │   └── cors.ts             # CORS configuration\n│   ├── modules/                # Feature modules (domain-driven)\n│   │   ├── auth/               # Authentication module\n│   │   │   ├── auth.controller.ts\n│   │   │   ├── auth.service.ts\n│   │   │   ├── auth.routes.ts\n│   │   │   ├── auth.middleware.ts\n│   │   │   └── auth.validation.ts\n│   │   ├── user/               # User management module\n│   │   │   ├── user.controller.ts\n│   │   │   ├── user.service.ts\n│   │   │   ├── user.routes.ts\n│   │   │   └── user.validation.ts\n│   │   ├── project/            # Project module\n│   │   │   ├── project.controller.ts\n│   │   │   ├── project.service.ts\n│   │   │   ├── project.routes.ts\n│   │   │   └── project.validation.ts\n│   │   ├── application/        # Application (apply) module\n│   │   │   ├── application.controller.ts\n│   │   │   ├── application.service.ts\n│   │   │   ├── application.routes.ts\n│   │   │   └── application.validation.ts\n│   │   ├── milestone/          # Milestone tracking module\n│   │   │   ├── milestone.controller.ts\n│   │   │   ├── milestone.service.ts\n│   │   │   ├── milestone.routes.ts\n│   │   │   └── milestone.validation.ts\n│   │   ├── matching/           # Matching module\n│   │   │   ├── matching.controller.ts\n│   │   │   ├── matching.service.ts\n│   │   │   └── matching.routes.ts\n│   │   ├── escrow/             # Escrow (simulated) module\n│   │   │   ├── escrow.controller.ts\n│   │   │   ├── escrow.service.ts\n│   │   │   └── escrow.routes.ts\n│   │   ├── portfolio/          # Verified Portfolio module\n│   │   │   ├── portfolio.controller.ts\n│   │   │   ├── portfolio.service.ts\n│   │   │   └── portfolio.routes.ts\n│   │   └── certificate/        # Certificate generation module\n│   │       ├── certificate.controller.ts\n│   │       ├── certificate.service.ts\n│   │       └── certificate.routes.ts\n│   ├── middleware/             # Shared middleware\n│   │   ├── auth.middleware.ts  # JWT verification\n│   │   ├── role.middleware.ts  # Role-based access control\n│   │   ├── validation.middleware.ts  # Request validation\n│   │   └── error.middleware.ts # Global error handler\n│   ├── utils/                  # Shared utilities\n│   │   ├── logger.ts           # Structured logging\n│   │   ├── response.ts         # Standardized API response\n│   │   └── errors.ts           # Custom error classes\n│   ├── prisma/                 # Prisma ORM\n│   │   ├── schema.prisma       # Database schema\n│   │   └── seed.ts             # Seed data for development\n│   ├── app.ts                  # Express app setup\n│   └── server.ts               # Server entry point\n├── tests/                      # Test files\n│   ├── unit/                   # Unit tests\n│   └── integration/            # Integration tests\n├── tsconfig.json\n├── package.json\n├── .env                        # Environment variables\n└── .env.example                # Environment template\n\`\`\`\n\n### 6.2 Layered Architecture\n\nThe backend follows a strict **3-layer architecture** within each module:\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Request Flow\"\n        Request[\"HTTP Request\"] --> Router[\"Router\"]\n        Router --> Middleware[\"Middleware<br>(Auth, Validation)\"]\n        Middleware --> Controller[\"Controller<br>(Request/Response handling)\"]\n        Controller --> Service[\"Service<br>(Business Logic)\"]\n        Service --> DAL[\"Data Access Layer<br>(Prisma ORM)\"]\n        DAL --> DB[\"PostgreSQL\"]\n    end\n\n    style Controller fill:#4A90D9,color:#fff\n    style Service fill:#7B68EE,color:#fff\n    style DAL fill:#2ECC71,color:#fff\n\`\`\`\n\n| Layer            | Responsibility                                                      |\n| :--------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------ |\n| **Controller**   | Parse request, call service, format response. No business logic.    |\n| **Service**      | All business logic. Orchestrates data access and domain operations.  |\n| **Data Access**  | Database queries via Prisma ORM. No business logic.                  |\n\n> [!IMPORTANT]\n> **Rules**: Controllers MUST NOT access the database directly. Services MUST NOT access `req` or `res` objects. This separation ensures testability and maintainability.\n\n### 6.3 Middleware Stack\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\ngraph LR\n    A[\"CORS\"] --> B[\"Body Parser\"]\n    B --> C[\"Request Logger\"]\n    C --> D[\"Rate Limiter\"]\n    D --> E[\"Route Handler\"]\n    E --> F[\"Error Handler\"]\n\`\`\`\n\n| Middleware            | Purpose                                                        |\n| :-------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **CORS**              | Allow frontend origin to access API                            |\n| **Body Parser**       | Parse JSON request bodies                                      |\n| **Request Logger**    | Log incoming requests (method, path, status, duration)         |\n| **Rate Limiter**      | Prevent abuse (100 requests/minute per IP)                     |\n| **Auth Middleware**    | Verify JWT token, attach user to request                       |\n| **Role Middleware**    | Check user role permissions for protected routes               |\n| **Validation**        | Validate request body/params using Zod/Joi schemas             |\n| **Error Handler**     | Catch and format all errors consistently                       |\n\n### 6.4 Error Handling Strategy\n\n\`\`\`typescript\n// Standardized error response format\ninterface ApiErrorResponse {\n  success: false;\n  error: {\n    code: string;         // e.g., \"VALIDATION_ERROR\", \"NOT_FOUND\"\n    message: string;      // Human-readable message\n    details?: unknown;    // Additional context (validation errors, etc.)\n  };\n}\n\n// Custom error classes\nclass AppError extends Error {\n  constructor(\n    public statusCode: number,\n    public code: string,\n    message: string,\n    public details?: unknown\n  ) {\n    super(message);\n  }\n}\n\nclass NotFoundError extends AppError {\n  constructor(resource: string) {\n    super(404, 'NOT_FOUND', `${resource} not found`);\n  }\n}\n\nclass ValidationError extends AppError {\n  constructor(details: unknown) {\n    super(400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Invalid input', details);\n  }\n}\n\nclass UnauthorizedError extends AppError {\n  constructor(message = 'Unauthorized') {\n    super(401, 'UNAUTHORIZED', message);\n  }\n}\n\nclass ForbiddenError extends AppError {\n  constructor(message = 'Forbidden') {\n    super(403, 'FORBIDDEN', message);\n  }\n}\n\`\`\`\n\n---\n\n## 7. Database Design\n\n### 7.1 Schema Overview\n\nThe database uses **PostgreSQL** with **Prisma ORM** for schema management and migrations.\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\nerDiagram\n    users ||--o| student_profiles : has\n    users ||--o| sme_profiles : has\n    sme_profiles ||--o{ projects : posts\n    projects ||--o{ milestones : contains\n    projects ||--o{ applications : receives\n    student_profiles ||--o{ applications : submits\n    student_profiles ||--o{ student_group_members : belongs_to\n    student_groups ||--o{ student_group_members : has\n    student_profiles ||--o{ portfolio_entries : earns\n    student_profiles ||--o{ certificates : receives\n    projects ||--o| portfolio_entries : generates\n    projects ||--o| certificates : generates\n\n    users {\n        uuid id PK\n        string email UK\n        string password_hash\n        enum role\n        boolean is_active\n        datetime created_at\n        datetime updated_at\n    }\n\n    student_profiles {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid user_id FK UK\n        string full_name\n        string university\n        string major\n        int year\n        text bio\n        json skills\n        string github_url\n        string avatar_url\n    }\n\n    sme_profiles {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid user_id FK UK\n        string company_name\n        string industry\n        text description\n        string contact_phone\n        string website\n        string logo_url\n    }\n\n    projects {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid sme_id FK\n        string title\n        text description\n        json required_skills\n        decimal budget\n        int duration_weeks\n        enum status\n        enum escrow_status\n        int max_team_size\n        datetime deadline\n        datetime created_at\n        datetime updated_at\n    }\n\n    milestones {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        string title\n        text description\n        int order_index\n        datetime deadline\n        enum status\n        text deliverable_url\n        text feedback\n    }\n\n    applications {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        uuid student_id FK\n        uuid group_id FK\n        text cover_message\n        enum status\n        datetime created_at\n    }\n\n    student_groups {\n        uuid id PK\n        string name\n        uuid leader_id FK\n        datetime created_at\n    }\n\n    student_group_members {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid group_id FK\n        uuid student_id FK\n    }\n\n    portfolio_entries {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid student_id FK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        string project_title\n        text project_description\n        string role_description\n        text deliverables_summary\n        datetime completed_at\n        boolean is_verified\n    }\n\n    certificates {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid student_id FK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        string student_name\n        string project_title\n        string sme_name\n        datetime issued_at\n        string verification_code UK\n    }\n\`\`\`\n\n### 7.2 Index Strategy\n\n| Table              | Index                                        | Type     | Purpose                             |\n| :----------------- | :------------------------------------------- | :------- | :---------------------------------- |\n| `users`            | `email`                                      | Unique   | Fast login lookup                   |\n| `projects`         | `status`                                     | B-Tree   | Filter by project status            |\n| `projects`         | `sme_id`                                     | B-Tree   | SME's project list                  |\n| `projects`         | `required_skills` (GIN)                      | GIN      | Skill-based search & matching       |\n| `applications`     | `(project_id, student_id)`                   | Unique   | Prevent duplicate applications      |\n| `applications`     | `student_id`                                 | B-Tree   | Student's application history       |\n| `milestones`       | `project_id`                                 | B-Tree   | Project milestone list              |\n| `portfolio_entries`| `student_id`                                 | B-Tree   | Student portfolio listing           |\n| `certificates`     | `verification_code`                          | Unique   | Certificate verification lookup     |\n\n### 7.3 Migration Strategy\n\n- Use **Prisma Migrate** for schema versioning.\n- Each migration is stored in `prisma/migrations/`.\n- Development: `npx prisma migrate dev`.\n- Production: `npx prisma migrate deploy`.\n- Seed data for development/testing: `npx prisma db seed`.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Authentication & Authorization\n\n### 8.1 Authentication Flow\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant User\n    participant Frontend\n    participant API as API Server\n    participant DB\n\n    User->>Frontend: Enter credentials\n    Frontend->>API: POST /api/auth/login {email, password}\n    API->>DB: Find user by email\n    DB-->>API: User record\n    API->>API: Verify password (bcrypt compare)\n    API-->>Frontend: {token: JWT, user: {...}}\n    Frontend->>Frontend: Store token in localStorage\n\n    Note over Frontend,API: Subsequent requests include JWT\n\n    Frontend->>API: GET /api/projects (Authorization: Bearer <token>)\n    API->>API: Verify JWT, extract user info\n    API->>DB: Query with user context\n    DB-->>API: Data\n    API-->>Frontend: Response\n\`\`\`\n\n### 8.2 JWT Token Structure\n\n\`\`\`json\n{\n  \"header\": {\n    \"alg\": \"HS256\",\n    \"typ\": \"JWT\"\n  },\n  \"payload\": {\n    \"sub\": \"user-uuid\",\n    \"email\": \"user@example.com\",\n    \"role\": \"STUDENT | SME | ADMIN\",\n    \"iat\": 1719792000,\n    \"exp\": 1719878400\n  }\n}\n\`\`\`\n\n### 8.3 Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)\n\n| Endpoint Category     | Student | SME   | Admin |\n| :-------------------- | :-----: | :---: | :---: |\n| View Projects         | ✅      | ✅    | ✅    |\n| Create Project        | ❌      | ✅    | ✅    |\n| Apply to Project      | ✅      | ❌    | ❌    |\n| Review Applications   | ❌      | ✅    | ✅    |\n| Confirm Match         | ❌      | ✅    | ✅    |\n| Submit Milestone      | ✅      | ❌    | ❌    |\n| Approve Milestone     | ❌      | ✅    | ✅    |\n| Accept Project        | ❌      | ✅    | ✅    |\n| View Portfolio        | ✅      | ✅    | ✅    |\n| Manage Users          | ❌      | ❌    | ✅    |\n| Moderate Content      | ❌      | ❌    | ✅    |\n\n---\n\n## 9. API Design\n\n### 9.1 API Conventions\n\n| Convention              | Standard                                           |\n| :---------------------- | :------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Base URL**            | `/api/v1`                                          |\n| **Method Semantics**    | `GET` = read, `POST` = create, `PUT` = update, `DELETE` = delete |\n| **Naming**              | Plural nouns: `/projects`, `/applications`         |\n| **Response Format**     | JSON with consistent envelope                      |\n| **Status Codes**        | 200 OK, 201 Created, 400 Bad Request, 401 Unauthorized, 403 Forbidden, 404 Not Found, 500 Internal Server Error |\n| **Pagination**          | `?page=1&limit=10` → response includes `meta: { total, page, limit, totalPages }` |\n| **Filtering**           | Query params: `?status=PUBLISHED&skills=react,nodejs` |\n| **Sorting**             | `?sort=created_at&order=desc`                      |\n\n### 9.2 Response Envelope\n\n\`\`\`typescript\n// Success Response\ninterface ApiSuccessResponse<T> {\n  success: true;\n  data: T;\n  meta?: {\n    total: number;\n    page: number;\n    limit: number;\n    totalPages: number;\n  };\n}\n\n// Error Response\ninterface ApiErrorResponse {\n  success: false;\n  error: {\n    code: string;\n    message: string;\n    details?: unknown;\n  };\n}\n\`\`\`\n\n### 9.3 Endpoint Catalog\n\n#### Authentication\n\n| Method | Endpoint                | Description              | Auth Required |\n| :----- | :---------------------- | :----------------------- | :-----------: |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/auth/register` | Register a new user      | No            |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/auth/login`    | Login and receive JWT    | No            |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/auth/refresh`  | Refresh JWT token        | Yes           |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/auth/forgot-password` | Request password reset | No         |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/auth/reset-password`  | Reset password with token | No       |\n\n#### Users & Profiles\n\n| Method | Endpoint                        | Description                        | Auth Required | Role        |\n| :----- | :------------------------------ | :--------------------------------- | :-----------: | :---------- |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/users/me`              | Get current user profile           | Yes           | Any         |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/users/me`              | Update current user profile        | Yes           | Any         |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/students/:id`          | Get student public profile         | Yes           | Any         |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/students/me/profile`   | Update student profile details     | Yes           | Student     |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/smes/:id`              | Get SME public profile             | Yes           | Any         |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/smes/me/profile`       | Update SME profile details         | Yes           | SME         |\n\n#### Projects\n\n| Method | Endpoint                           | Description                        | Auth Required | Role        |\n| :----- | :--------------------------------- | :--------------------------------- | :-----------: | :---------- |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/projects`                 | List published projects (filterable) | Yes         | Any         |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/projects/:id`             | Get project details                | Yes           | Any         |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/projects`                 | Create a new project               | Yes           | SME         |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/projects/:id`             | Update project details             | Yes           | SME (owner) |\n| DELETE | `/api/v1/projects/:id`             | Cancel/delete project              | Yes           | SME (owner) |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/projects/:id/status`      | Update project status              | Yes           | Admin       |\n\n#### Applications\n\n| Method | Endpoint                                    | Description                        | Auth Required | Role        |\n| :----- | :------------------------------------------ | :--------------------------------- | :-----------: | :---------- |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/projects/:id/applications`         | Apply to a project                 | Yes           | Student     |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/projects/:id/applications`         | List applications for a project    | Yes           | SME (owner) |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/students/me/applications`          | List student's own applications    | Yes           | Student     |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/applications/:id/status`           | Accept/reject application          | Yes           | SME         |\n| DELETE | `/api/v1/applications/:id`                  | Withdraw application               | Yes           | Student     |\n\n#### Matching\n\n| Method | Endpoint                                    | Description                        | Auth Required | Role        |\n| :----- | :------------------------------------------ | :--------------------------------- | :-----------: | :---------- |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/projects/:id/recommendations`      | Get recommended students for project | Yes         | SME / Admin |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/projects/:id/match`                | Confirm match with selected group  | Yes           | SME         |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/projects/:id/match/confirm`        | Student confirms match acceptance  | Yes           | Student     |\n\n#### Milestones\n\n| Method | Endpoint                                    | Description                        | Auth Required | Role        |\n| :----- | :------------------------------------------ | :--------------------------------- | :-----------: | :---------- |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/projects/:id/milestones`           | List milestones for a project      | Yes           | Any (member)|\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/milestones/:id/submit`             | Submit milestone for review        | Yes           | Student     |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/milestones/:id/review`             | Approve/request revision           | Yes           | SME         |\n\n#### Acceptance & Portfolio\n\n| Method | Endpoint                                    | Description                        | Auth Required | Role        |\n| :----- | :------------------------------------------ | :--------------------------------- | :-----------: | :---------- |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/projects/:id/accept`               | Accept project deliverables        | Yes           | SME         |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/students/:id/portfolio`            | Get student's verified portfolio   | Yes           | Any         |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/certificates/:code`                | Verify certificate by code         | No            | Public      |\n\n#### Admin\n\n| Method | Endpoint                                    | Description                        | Auth Required | Role        |\n| :----- | :------------------------------------------ | :--------------------------------- | :-----------: | :---------- |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/admin/projects/pending`            | List projects pending review       | Yes           | Admin       |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/admin/projects/:id/approve`        | Approve project for publishing     | Yes           | Admin       |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/admin/projects/:id/reject`         | Reject project with reason         | Yes           | Admin       |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/admin/stats`                       | Platform KPI dashboard             | Yes           | Admin       |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Deployment Architecture\n\n### 10.1 MVP Deployment\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Developer\"\n        Dev[\"Local Development\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph \"GitHub\"\n        Repo[\"GitHub Repository\"]\n        Actions[\"GitHub Actions<br>(CI/CD)\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph \"Production\"\n        Vercel[\"Vercel<br>(Frontend)\"]\n        Railway[\"Railway<br>(Backend + PostgreSQL)\"]\n    end\n\n    Dev -->|git push| Repo\n    Repo -->|trigger| Actions\n    Actions -->|deploy frontend| Vercel\n    Actions -->|deploy backend| Railway\n\`\`\`\n\n### 10.2 Environment Configuration\n\n| Environment   | Frontend          | Backend             | Database             |\n| :------------ | :---------------- | :------------------ | :------------------- |\n| **Development** | localhost:3000  | localhost:4000      | localhost:5432       |\n| **Staging**   | staging.skillbridge.vn | api-staging.skillbridge.vn | Railway (staging) |\n| **Production**| skillbridge.vn   | api.skillbridge.vn  | Railway (production) |\n\n### 10.3 Environment Variables\n\n\`\`\`bash\n# Backend .env\nNODE_ENV=production\nPORT=4000\nDATABASE_URL=postgresql://user:pass@host:5432/skillbridge\nJWT_SECRET=<random-64-char-string>\nJWT_EXPIRES_IN=24h\nCORS_ORIGIN=https://skillbridge.vn\nSMTP_HOST=smtp.gmail.com\nSMTP_PORT=587\nSMTP_USER=noreply@skillbridge.vn\nSMTP_PASS=<app-password>\nCLOUDINARY_URL=cloudinary://key:secret@cloud\n\n# Frontend .env.local\nNEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=https://api.skillbridge.vn/api/v1\nNEXT_PUBLIC_APP_NAME=SkillBridge\n\`\`\`\n\n---\n\n## 11. Security Architecture\n\n### 11.1 Security Measures\n\n| Threat                  | Mitigation                                                        |\n| :---------------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **SQL Injection**       | Prisma ORM (parameterized queries); no raw SQL                    |\n| **XSS**                | React auto-escaping; Content-Security-Policy headers              |\n| **CSRF**               | SameSite cookies; token-based auth (JWT) instead of session cookies |\n| **Brute Force Login**  | Rate limiting on auth endpoints (5 attempts/minute)                |\n| **Data Exposure**      | Role-based API access; query scoping by user ownership             |\n| **Sensitive Data**     | bcrypt password hashing; JWT secret rotation                       |\n| **HTTPS**              | Enforced via Vercel/Railway (automatic SSL)                        |\n| **Dependency Vulnerabilities** | `npm audit` in CI; Dependabot alerts on GitHub              |\n\n### 11.2 Security Headers\n\n\`\`\`typescript\n// Applied via middleware (helmet.js)\n{\n  'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',\n  'X-Frame-Options': 'DENY',\n  'X-XSS-Protection': '1; mode=block',\n  'Strict-Transport-Security': 'max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains',\n  'Content-Security-Policy': \"default-src 'self'\",\n  'Referrer-Policy': 'strict-origin-when-cross-origin',\n}\n\`\`\`\n\n---\n\n## 12. Performance & Scalability\n\n### 12.1 MVP Performance Targets\n\n| Metric                    | Target       |\n| :------------------------ | :----------- |\n| Page load (First Contentful Paint) | ≤ 2s  |\n| API response (P95)        | ≤ 500ms      |\n| Concurrent users          | 100          |\n| Database queries per request | ≤ 5       |\n\n### 12.2 Optimization Strategies\n\n| Strategy                    | Application                                            |\n| :-------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Server-Side Rendering**   | Next.js SSR for initial page loads (SEO + performance) |\n| **Static Generation**       | Landing page, public portfolio pages                    |\n| **Database Indexing**       | Key lookup fields indexed (see Section 7.2)             |\n| **Query Optimization**      | Prisma `select` / `include` to avoid N+1 queries       |\n| **Image Optimization**      | Next.js `<Image>` component with Cloudinary CDN        |\n| **Caching**                 | HTTP cache headers for static assets                    |\n\n### 12.3 Scalability Path (Post-MVP)\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\ngraph LR\n    subgraph \"MVP (Monolith)\"\n        A[\"Single API Server\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph \"V1.1 (Optimized Monolith)\"\n        B[\"API Server + Redis Cache\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph \"V2.0 (Modular)\"\n        C[\"API Gateway\"]\n        D[\"Auth Service\"]\n        E[\"Project Service\"]\n        F[\"Matching Service (AI)\"]\n        G[\"Notification Service\"]\n    end\n\n    A -->|\"Growth\"| B\n    B -->|\"Scale\"| C\n    C --> D\n    C --> E\n    C --> F\n    C --> G\n\`\`\`\n\n---\n\n## 13. Monitoring & Logging\n\n### 13.1 Logging Strategy\n\n| Log Level | Usage                                            | Example                            |\n| :-------- | :----------------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------- |\n| `error`   | Unhandled exceptions, critical failures          | Database connection failed          |\n| `warn`    | Unexpected but recoverable situations            | Rate limit approaching              |\n| `info`    | Significant business events                      | User registered, project posted     |\n| `debug`   | Detailed technical information (dev/staging only) | SQL queries, request payloads      |\n\n### 13.2 Structured Log Format\n\n\`\`\`json\n{\n  \"timestamp\": \"2026-09-15T10:30:00.000Z\",\n  \"level\": \"info\",\n  \"message\": \"Project created\",\n  \"context\": {\n    \"userId\": \"uuid-123\",\n    \"projectId\": \"uuid-456\",\n    \"action\": \"project.create\"\n  },\n  \"requestId\": \"req-789\"\n}\n\`\`\`\n\n### 13.3 Monitoring Tools (MVP)\n\n| Tool                | Purpose                              | Tier     |\n| :------------------ | :----------------------------------- | :------- |\n| Railway Metrics     | Server CPU, memory, request count    | Free     |\n| Vercel Analytics    | Frontend performance & Core Web Vitals | Free   |\n| Sentry (Free tier)  | Error tracking & alerting           | Free     |\n| UptimeRobot         | Uptime monitoring & alerts          | Free     |\n\n---\n\n## 14. Evolution Roadmap\n\n### 14.1 Architecture Evolution by Phase\n\n| Aspect              | MVP (Q3/2026)               | V1.1 (Q4/2026)                    | V2.0 (Q1/2027)                    |\n| :------------------ | :-------------------------- | :--------------------------------- | :--------------------------------- |\n| **Architecture**    | Modular Monolith            | Optimized Monolith + Cache         | Service-Oriented Architecture      |\n| **Frontend**        | Next.js (Web)               | Next.js (Web, optimized)           | Next.js + React Native (Mobile)    |\n| **Backend**         | Express + Prisma            | + Redis + Queue (Bull)             | + API Gateway + Microservices      |\n| **Database**        | PostgreSQL                  | PostgreSQL + Redis cache           | PostgreSQL + Redis + Search engine |\n| **Auth**            | JWT (email/password)        | + Google OAuth                     | + SSO for university accounts      |\n| **Payments**        | Simulated escrow            | Real payment gateway (VNPay/Momo)  | Advanced escrow with milestones    |\n| **Matching**        | Skill-tag overlap (manual)  | Rule-based scoring                 | AI/ML matching engine              |\n| **File Storage**    | Cloudinary (free tier)      | AWS S3                             | AWS S3 + CDN                       |\n| **Notifications**   | Email (SMTP)                | Email + In-app                     | Email + In-app + Push (mobile)     |\n| **CI/CD**           | GitHub Actions              | + Staging environment              | + Automated testing pipeline       |\n| **Monitoring**      | Basic (Sentry + UptimeRobot)| + APM (Application Performance)    | Full observability stack           |\n\n### 14.2 Key Technical Decisions for Future Phases\n\n| Decision Point                    | MVP Decision           | Future Consideration                        |\n| :-------------------------------- | :--------------------- | :------------------------------------------ |\n| Monolith vs. Microservices        | Monolith               | Decompose when team > 5 or traffic > 10k MAU |\n| SQL vs. NoSQL                     | PostgreSQL (SQL)       | Add NoSQL (MongoDB) for unstructured data if needed |\n| REST vs. GraphQL                  | REST                   | Consider GraphQL for mobile app in V2.0      |\n| Server-rendered vs. SPA           | Next.js (hybrid)       | Keep hybrid approach                         |\n| Payment Gateway                   | Simulated              | VNPay / Momo integration in V1.1             |\n| Search Engine                     | PostgreSQL full-text   | Elasticsearch / Meilisearch in V2.0          |\n| Message Queue                     | None                   | BullMQ (Redis) for async jobs in V1.1        |\n\n---\n\n*End of System Architecture Document — SkillBridge MVP v1.0*\n", "Description": "System Architecture Document for SkillBridge MVP. Introduction\n\n### 1.1 Purpose\n\nThis document establishes the **coding conventions, standards, and architectural patterns** for the SkillBridge project. All team members MUST follow these guidelines to ensure:\n\n- **Consistency** — Code reads the same regardless of who wrote it.\n- **Maintainability** — Easy to understand, modify, and extend.\n- **Quality** — Fewer bugs through disciplined patterns and testing.\n- **Onboarding** — New developers can quickly understand the codebase.\n\n### 1.2 Scope\n\nThese conventions apply to all source code in the SkillBridge repository, including:\n- Frontend (Next.js / React / TypeScript)\n- Backend (Node.js / Express / TypeScript)\n- Database schema (Prisma)\n- Tests\n- Scripts and configuration files\n\n### 1.3 References\n\n- [Software Requirements Specification (SRS)](./SRS_MVP.md)\n- [System Architecture Document](./System_Architecture.md)\n- [Business Plan — SkillBridge (PA3)](./Business_Plan_Updated_PA3.md)\n\n### 1.4 Enforcement\n\n| Tool           | Purpose                                         |\n| :------------- | :---------------------------------------------- |\n| **ESLint**     | Static code analysis and style enforcement       |\n| **Prettier**   | Automatic code formatting                        |\n| **TypeScript** | Type checking (strict mode)                      |\n| **Husky**      | Pre-commit hooks (lint + format)                 |\n| **lint-staged**| Run linters only on staged files                 |\n| **PR Reviews** | Manual code review before merge                  |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Project Structure\n\n### 2.1 Repository Layout (Monorepo)\n\n\`\`\`\nskillbridge/\n├── frontend/                   # Next.js frontend application\n├── backend/                    # Express backend application\n├── docs/                       #
