Community Review System, Two-way rating\n- Talent Pool, Progress Log (detailed)\n- AI Matching, Mobile App, University API\n\n### 1.3 MVP Functions\n\n| # | Function                         | Description                                                    |\n|---|----------------------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------|\n| 1 | Project Posting (SME)            | SMEs create structured project listings                        |\n| 2 | Student Skill Profile            | Students create profiles with skills and experience            |\n| 3 | Project Application              | Students apply individually or as groups (2–4)                 |\n| 4 | Semi-automatic Matching          | Skill-tag based recommendation + manual admin support          |\n| 5 | Milestone Tracking               | Basic progress tracking with defined milestones                |\n| 6 | Escrow Payment (Simulated)       | Status simulation only (no real gateway)                       |\n| 7 | Acceptance Mechanism             | SME reviews and approves deliverables                          |\n| 8 | Verified Portfolio & Certificate | Auto-update portfolio + issue digital certificate              |\n\n### 1.4 Glossary\n\n| Term                   | Definition                                                              |\n| :--------------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **SME**                | Small and Medium Enterprise — demand side                               |\n| **Student**            | University student (Year 2–4) — supply side                             |\n| **Project**            | Short-term task (1–4 weeks), budget ~1–5M VND                           |\n| **Milestone**          | Checkpoint within a project with deliverables and deadline              |\n| **Escrow (Simulated)** | Simulated payment holding (no real payment gateway in MVP)              |\n| **Matching**           | Connecting a student/group with a suitable project                      |\n| **Verified Portfolio** | Student's portfolio of completed projects, verified by the platform     |\n| **Acceptance**         | SME reviews and approves final deliverables                             |\n\n### 1.5 References\n\n- [Business Plan — SkillBridge (PA3)](./Business_Plan_Updated_PA3.md)\n- [System Architecture Document](./System_Architecture.md)\n- [Coding Standards](./Source_Code_Documentation.md)\n\n---\n\n## 2. User Roles\n\n### 2.1 Student (Supply Side)\n\n| Aspect              | Details                                                           |\n| :------------------ | :---------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Characteristics** | Year 2–4, IT/Business major, tech-savvy, mobile & web user       |\n| **Registration**    | Email, university name, major, year                               |\n| **Profile**         | Skills, interests, past projects, GitHub links                    |\n| **Actions**         | Browse projects, apply (individual/group), execute milestones, receive certificate |\n\n### 2.2 SME (Demand Side)\n\n| Aspect              | Details                                                           |\n| :------------------ | :---------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Characteristics** | Small business owner/founder, limited tech expertise, budget-conscious |\n| **Registration**    | Company name, industry, contact info                              |\n| **Actions**         | Post projects, review applications, select student/group, track milestones, accept deliverables |\n\n### 2.3 Admin (Platform Operator)\n\n| Aspect              | Details                                                           |\n| :------------------ | :---------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Characteristics** | Founding team member                                              |\n| **Actions**         | Moderate content, facilitate matching, manage users, monitor KPIs |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Functional Requirements\n\n### 3.1 Authentication & Authorization\n\n| ID         | Requirement                                                            | Priority |\n| :--------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-AUTH-01 | Users SHALL register as either **Student** or **SME**                  | Must     |\n| FR-AUTH-02 | Users SHALL authenticate via email/password                            | Must     |\n| FR-AUTH-03 | The system SHOULD support Google OAuth                                 | Should   |\n| FR-AUTH-04 | The system SHALL issue JWT tokens for session management               | Must     |\n| FR-AUTH-05 | The system SHALL enforce role-based access control (Student, SME, Admin)| Must    |\n| FR-AUTH-06 | Users SHALL be able to reset passwords via email                       | Must     |\n\n### 3.2 Student Profile Management\n\n| ID         | Requirement                                                            | Priority |\n| :--------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-PROF-01 | Students SHALL create and edit their profile                           | Must     |\n| FR-PROF-02 | Profile SHALL include: full name, university, major, year, bio, skills (tags), GitHub links | Must |\n| FR-PROF-03 | Profile SHALL display the student's Verified Portfolio                  | Must     |\n| FR-PROF-04 | Profile SHOULD display skill-level indicators                          | Should   |\n| FR-PROF-05 | Profile SHALL be viewable by SMEs during application review            | Must     |\n\n### 3.3 Project Posting (SME)\n\n| ID         | Requirement                                                            | Priority |\n| :--------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-PROJ-01 | SMEs SHALL create project postings                                     | Must     |\n| FR-PROJ-02 | Posting SHALL include: title, description, required skills, budget (VND), duration, milestones, deliverables | Must |\n| FR-PROJ-03 | Posting SHALL include milestones with descriptions and deadlines       | Must     |\n| FR-PROJ-04 | Posting SHALL require Admin approval before publishing                 | Must     |\n| FR-PROJ-05 | SMEs SHALL edit projects before matching                               | Must     |\n| FR-PROJ-06 | SMEs SHALL cancel unmatched projects                                   | Must     |\n| FR-PROJ-07 | The system SHOULD categorize projects by skill area                    | Should   |\n\n### 3.4 Project Application (Student)\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                           | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-APPLY-01 | Students SHALL browse published projects                              | Must     |\n| FR-APPLY-02 | Students SHALL filter/search by skill, category, budget, duration     | Must     |\n| FR-APPLY-03 | Students SHALL apply individually                                     | Must     |\n| FR-APPLY-04 | Students SHALL apply as a group (2–4 members)                         | Must     |\n| FR-APPLY-05 | Application SHOULD include a cover message                            | Should   |\n| FR-APPLY-06 | Students SHALL withdraw applications before matching                  | Must     |\n\n### 3.5 Matching\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                           | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-MATCH-01 | System SHALL recommend students/groups based on skill-tag overlap      | Must     |\n| FR-MATCH-02 | SMEs SHALL review recommended profiles                                | Must     |\n| FR-MATCH-03 | SMEs SHALL select and confirm a student/group                         | Must     |\n| FR-MATCH-04 | System SHALL notify selected student/group                            | Must     |\n| FR-MATCH-05 | Student/group SHALL confirm match acceptance                          | Must     |\n| FR-MATCH-06 | Admin SHALL be able to manually intervene in matching                  | Must     |\n\n### 3.6 Milestone Tracking\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                           | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-MILE-01  | Each project SHALL have predefined milestones                         | Must     |\n| FR-MILE-02  | Milestones SHALL have: title, description, deadline, status (Not Started / In Progress / Submitted / Approved) | Must |\n| FR-MILE-03  | Students SHALL submit milestones for review                           | Must     |\n| FR-MILE-04  | SMEs SHALL approve/reject submitted milestones                        | Must     |\n| FR-MILE-05  | System SHALL display milestone overview for both parties              | Must     |\n| FR-MILE-06  | System SHOULD send notifications on milestone events                  | Should   |\n\n### 3.7 Escrow Payment (Simulated)\n\n| ID         | Requirement                                                            | Priority |\n| :--------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-ESC-01  | System SHALL simulate escrow status (Pending → Deposited → Disbursed) | Must     |\n| FR-ESC-02  | Match confirmation SHALL trigger \"Deposited\" status                   | Must     |\n| FR-ESC-03  | Project acceptance SHALL trigger \"Disbursed\" status                   | Must     |\n| FR-ESC-04  | Escrow status SHALL be visible to both SME and Student                | Must     |\n| FR-ESC-05  | No real money transaction SHALL occur in MVP                          | Must     |\n\n### 3.8 Acceptance Mechanism\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                           | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-ACPT-01  | SMEs SHALL review final deliverables                                  | Must     |\n| FR-ACPT-02  | SMEs SHALL approve or request revisions                               | Must     |\n| FR-ACPT-03  | Acceptance SHALL trigger: escrow disbursement, portfolio update, certificate | Must |\n| FR-ACPT-04  | System SHALL mark project as \"Completed\" after acceptance             | Must     |\n\n### 3.9 Verified Portfolio & Certificate\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                           | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-PORT-01  | Acceptance SHALL auto-add project to student's Verified Portfolio      | Must     |\n| FR-PORT-02  | Portfolio entry SHALL include: title, description, role, deliverables, completion date | Must |\n| FR-PORT-03  | System SHALL generate a digital certificate (PDF or in-app)           | Must     |\n| FR-PORT-04  | Certificate SHALL include: student name, project title, SME name, date, verification badge | Must |\n| FR-PORT-05  | Students SHALL view and share their portfolio publicly                | Must     |\n\n### 3.10 Notifications\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                           | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| FR-NOTIF-01 | System SHOULD send email notifications for key events                 | Should   |\n| FR-NOTIF-02 | System SHOULD display in-app notifications                            | Should   |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Non-Functional Requirements\n\n### 4.1 Performance\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                           | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| NFR-PERF-01 | Page load time SHALL be ≤ 3 seconds                                  | Must     |\n| NFR-PERF-02 | API response time SHALL be ≤ 500ms (P95)                             | Must     |\n| NFR-PERF-03 | System SHALL support ≥ 100 concurrent users                          | Must     |\n\n### 4.2 Security\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                           | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| NFR-SEC-01  | Passwords SHALL be hashed (bcrypt or equivalent)                     | Must     |\n| NFR-SEC-02  | All API endpoints SHALL enforce auth and authorization               | Must     |\n| NFR-SEC-03  | All communications SHALL use HTTPS                                   | Must     |\n| NFR-SEC-04  | JWT tokens SHALL have configurable expiration (default: 24h)         | Must     |\n| NFR-SEC-05  | System SHALL protect against XSS, CSRF, SQL Injection                | Must     |\n\n### 4.3 Scalability\n\n| ID           | Requirement                                                          | Priority |\n| :----------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| NFR-SCAL-01  | Architecture SHALL support horizontal scaling                        | Should   |\n| NFR-SCAL-02  | Database design SHALL enable future microservice decomposition       | Should   |\n\n### 4.4 Usability\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                           | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| NFR-USE-01  | UI SHALL be responsive on desktop and mobile browsers                | Must     |\n| NFR-USE-02  | Key flows SHALL complete in ≤ 5 steps                                | Should   |\n| NFR-USE-03  | System SHALL provide clear error messages and validation feedback    | Must     |\n| NFR-USE-04  | Primary language: Vietnamese; secondary: English                     | Must     |\n\n### 4.5 Reliability\n\n| ID          | Requirement                                                           | Priority |\n| :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| NFR-REL-01  | System SHALL target 99% uptime during Pilot                          | Must     |\n| NFR-REL-02  | System SHALL implement daily automated backups                       | Must     |\n\n### 4.6 Maintainability\n\n| ID           | Requirement                                                          | Priority |\n| :----------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------- | :------: |\n| NFR-MAIN-01  | Codebase SHALL follow [Coding Standards](./Source_Code_Documentation.md) | Must  |\n| NFR-MAIN-02  | Unit test coverage SHALL be ≥ 60% for core modules                   | Should   |\n| NFR-MAIN-03  | System SHALL use structured logging                                  | Should   |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Use Cases\n\n### 5.1 UC-01: SME Posts a New Project\n\n| Field             | Description                                                                   |\n| :---------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Actor**         | SME                                                                           |\n| **Precondition**  | SME is registered and logged in                                               |\n| **Main Flow**     | 1. SME navigates to \"Post Project\"<br>2. Fills in details (title, description, skills, budget, duration, milestones)<br>3. Submits the project<br>4. Admin reviews and approves<br>5. Project is published |\n| **Postcondition** | Project is visible to students                                                |\n| **Alternate Flow**| 4a. Admin rejects → SME notified with reason → can edit and resubmit          |\n\n### 5.2 UC-02: Student Applies to a Project\n\n| Field             | Description                                                                   |\n| :---------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Actor**         | Student                                                                       |\n| **Precondition**  | Student is registered, logged in, profile is complete                         |\n| **Main Flow**     | 1. Browses project listings<br>2. Selects a project<br>3. Applies with optional cover message<br>4. Application recorded and visible to SME |\n| **Postcondition** | Application appears in SME's review queue                                     |\n| **Alternate Flow**| 3a. Apply as group → invite students → all linked to application              |\n\n### 5.3 UC-03: Matching — SME Selects a Student/Group\n\n| Field             | Description                                                                   |\n| :---------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Actor**         | SME, Admin                                                                    |\n| **Precondition**  | At least one student/group has applied                                        |\n| **Main Flow**     | 1. SME reviews applications and recommendations<br>2. Selects a student/group<br>3. System notifies selected<br>4. Student/group confirms<br>5. Status → \"In Progress\"; escrow → \"Deposited\" |\n| **Postcondition** | Project matched and ready for execution                                       |\n| **Alternate Flow**| 4a. Student declines → SME selects another applicant                          |\n\n### 5.4 UC-04: Milestone Tracking & Submission\n\n| Field             | Description                                                                   |\n| :---------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Actor**         | Student, SME                                                                  |\n| **Precondition**  | Project is matched and in progress                                            |\n| **Main Flow**     | 1. Student works on current milestone<br>2. Submits milestone (\"Submitted\")<br>3. SME reviews deliverables<br>4. SME approves (\"Approved\") |\n| **Postcondition** | Milestone completed; next milestone becomes active                            |\n| **Alternate Flow**| 4a. SME requests revisions → student reworks and resubmits                    |\n\n### 5.5 UC-05: Project Acceptance & Certificate\n\n| Field             | Description                                                                   |\n| :---------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Actor**         | SME, System                                                                   |\n| **Precondition**  | All milestones approved                                                       |\n| **Main Flow**     | 1. SME reviews final deliverables<br>2. SME approves<br>3. Escrow → \"Disbursed\"<br>4. Portfolio updated<br>5. Certificate generated |\n| **Postcondition** | Project \"Completed\"; student has portfolio entry and certificate              |\n\n---\n\n## 6. Data Models\n\n### 6.1 Entity-Relationship Diagram\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\nerDiagram\n    USER ||--o| STUDENT_PROFILE : has\n    USER ||--o| SME_PROFILE : has\n    USER {\n        uuid id PK\n        string email\n        string password_hash\n        enum role \"STUDENT | SME | ADMIN\"\n        datetime created_at\n        datetime updated_at\n    }\n\n    STUDENT_PROFILE {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid user_id FK\n        string full_name\n        string university\n        string major\n        int year\n        text bio\n        json skills\n        string github_url\n    }\n\n    SME_PROFILE {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid user_id FK\n        string company_name\n        string industry\n        text description\n        string contact_phone\n        string website\n    }\n\n    PROJECT {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid sme_id FK\n        string title\n        text description\n        json required_skills\n        decimal budget\n        int duration_weeks\n        enum status\n        enum escrow_status\n        datetime created_at\n        datetime updated_at\n    }\n\n    MILESTONE {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        string title\n        text description\n        datetime deadline\n        int order_index\n        enum status\n    }\n\n    APPLICATION {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        uuid student_id FK\n        uuid group_id FK \"nullable\"\n        text cover_message\n        enum status\n        datetime created_at\n    }\n\n    STUDENT_GROUP {\n        uuid id PK\n        string name\n        uuid leader_id FK\n    }\n\n    STUDENT_GROUP_MEMBER {\n        uuid group_id FK\n        uuid student_id FK\n    }\n\n    VERIFIED_PORTFOLIO_ENTRY {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid student_id FK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        string project_title\n        text description\n        string role\n        datetime completed_at\n    }\n\n    CERTIFICATE {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid student_id FK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        string student_name\n        string project_title\n        string sme_name\n        datetime issued_at\n        string verification_code\n    }\n\n    SME_PROFILE ||--o{ PROJECT : posts\n    PROJECT ||--o{ MILESTONE : contains\n    PROJECT ||--o{ APPLICATION : receives\n    STUDENT_PROFILE ||--o{ APPLICATION : submits\n    STUDENT_GROUP ||--o{ STUDENT_GROUP_MEMBER : has\n    STUDENT_PROFILE ||--o{ STUDENT_GROUP_MEMBER : belongs_to\n    STUDENT_PROFILE ||--o{ VERIFIED_PORTFOLIO_ENTRY : earns\n    STUDENT_PROFILE ||--o{ CERTIFICATE : receives\n    PROJECT ||--o| VERIFIED_PORTFOLIO_ENTRY : generates\n    PROJECT ||--o| CERTIFICATE : generates\n\`\`\`\n\n### 6.2 Key Enumerations\n\n| Enum Name           | Values                                                                      |\n| :------------------ | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `UserRole`          | `STUDENT`, `SME`, `ADMIN`                                                   |\n| `ProjectStatus`     | `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `PUBLISHED`, `MATCHED`, `IN_PROGRESS`, `COMPLETED`, `CANCELLED` |\n| `EscrowStatus`      | `NONE`, `DEPOSITED`, `DISBURSED`                                            |\n| `MilestoneStatus`   | `NOT_STARTED`, `IN_PROGRESS`, `SUBMITTED`, `APPROVED`, `REVISION_REQUESTED` |\n| `ApplicationStatus` | `PENDING`, `ACCEPTED`, `REJECTED`, `WITHDRAWN`                              |\n\n---\n\n## 7. API Specification\n\n### 7.1 Authentication\n\n| Method | Endpoint                       | Description            | Auth |\n| :----- | :----------------------------- | :--------------------- | :--: |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/auth/register`        | Register new user      | No   |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/auth/login`           | Login, receive JWT     | No   |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/auth/refresh`         | Refresh JWT            | Yes  |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/auth/forgot-password` | Request password reset | No   |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/auth/reset-password`  | Reset password         | No   |\n\n### 7.2 Users & Profiles\n\n| Method | Endpoint                      | Description                  | Auth | Role    |\n| :----- | :---------------------------- | :--------------------------- | :--: | :------ |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/users/me`            | Get current user             | Yes  | Any     |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/users/me`            | Update current user          | Yes  | Any     |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/students/:id`        | Get student public profile   | Yes  | Any     |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/students/me/profile` | Update student profile       | Yes  | Student |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/smes/:id`            | Get SME public profile       | Yes  | Any     |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/smes/me/profile`     | Update SME profile           | Yes  | SME     |\n\n### 7.3 Projects\n\n| Method | Endpoint                      | Description                    | Auth | Role        |\n| :----- | :---------------------------- | :----------------------------- | :--: | :---------- |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/projects`            | List projects (filterable)     | Yes  | Any         |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/projects/:id`        | Get project details            | Yes  | Any         |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/projects`            | Create project                 | Yes  | SME         |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/projects/:id`        | Update project                 | Yes  | SME (owner) |\n| DELETE | `/api/v1/projects/:id`        | Cancel/delete project          | Yes  | SME (owner) |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/projects/:id/status` | Update project status          | Yes  | Admin       |\n\n### 7.4 Applications\n\n| Method | Endpoint                              | Description                  | Auth | Role        |\n| :----- | :------------------------------------ | :--------------------------- | :--: | :---------- |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/projects/:id/applications`   | Apply to project             | Yes  | Student     |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/projects/:id/applications`   | List applications for project| Yes  | SME (owner) |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/students/me/applications`    | List student's applications  | Yes  | Student     |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/applications/:id/status`     | Accept/reject application    | Yes  | SME         |\n| DELETE | `/api/v1/applications/:id`            | Withdraw application         | Yes  | Student     |\n\n### 7.5 Matching\n\n| Method | Endpoint                              | Description                    | Auth | Role      |\n| :----- | :------------------------------------ | :----------------------------- | :--: | :-------- |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/projects/:id/recommendations`| Get recommended students       | Yes  | SME/Admin |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/projects/:id/match`          | Confirm match                  | Yes  | SME       |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/projects/:id/match/confirm`  | Student confirms acceptance    | Yes  | Student   |\n\n### 7.6 Milestones\n\n| Method | Endpoint                        | Description              | Auth | Role     |\n| :----- | :------------------------------ | :----------------------- | :--: | :------- |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/projects/:id/milestones`| List project milestones | Yes  | Member   |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/milestones/:id/submit` | Submit for review        | Yes  | Student  |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/milestones/:id/review` | Approve/request revision | Yes  | SME      |\n\n### 7.7 Acceptance & Portfolio\n\n| Method | Endpoint                         | Description                  | Auth | Role   |\n| :----- | :------------------------------- | :--------------------------- | :--: | :----- |\n| POST   | `/api/v1/projects/:id/accept`    | Accept project deliverables  | Yes  | SME    |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/students/:id/portfolio` | Get verified portfolio       | Yes  | Any    |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/certificates/:code`     | Verify certificate by code   | No   | Public |\n\n### 7.8 Admin\n\n| Method | Endpoint                              | Description              | Auth | Role  |\n| :----- | :------------------------------------ | :----------------------- | :--: | :---- |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/admin/projects/pending`      | List pending projects    | Yes  | Admin |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/admin/projects/:id/approve`  | Approve project          | Yes  | Admin |\n| PUT    | `/api/v1/admin/projects/:id/reject`   | Reject project           | Yes  | Admin |\n| GET    | `/api/v1/admin/stats`                 | Platform KPI dashboard   | Yes  | Admin |\n\n---\n\n## 8. External Interfaces\n\n### 8.1 Key Screens\n\nHome, Registration/Login, Student Dashboard, SME Dashboard, Project Listing/Search, Project Detail/Apply, Milestone Tracking, Profile Page (Student/SME), Admin Panel.\n\n### 8.2 External Services\n\n| Service        | Purpose                              | MVP Priority |\n| :------------- | :----------------------------------- | :----------: |\n| Email (SMTP)   | Notification emails                  | Should       |\n| Google OAuth   | Social login                         | Should       |\n| Cloud Storage  | Profile pictures, uploads            | Should       |\n| PDF Generation | Completion certificates              | Must         |\n\n### 8.3 Collaboration Tools (MVP Workaround)\n\n| Tool         | Purpose                       |\n| :----------- | :---------------------------- |\n| Google Drive | File sharing                  |\n| GitHub       | Source code collaboration     |\n| Discord      | Real-time communication       |\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> External tools are temporary. Integrated workspace planned for V1.1.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Constraints & Assumptions\n\n### 9.1 Constraints\n\n1. **Budget**: Minimal; prefer free-tier cloud services\n2. **Team Size**: 2 developers (CTO + CEO)\n3. **Timeline**: MVP deployable by end of **September 2026** (Q3)\n4. **No Real Payments**: Escrow simulated via status flags\n5. **Pilot Scale**: ≤ 25 students, ≤ 8 SMEs, ≤ 10 projects in 8 weeks\n\n### 9.2 Assumptions\n\n1. Students have reliable internet and modern browsers\n2. SMEs can articulate project requirements in structured form\n3. Admin (founding team) available for manual matching/moderation during Pilot\n4. External collaboration tools are familiar to both user groups\n5. Email (SMTP) is sufficient for MVP notifications\n\n### 9.3 Risks\n\n| Risk                                    | Impact | Mitigation                                                |\n| :-------------------------------------- | :----: | :-------------------------------------------------------- |\n| Low SME engagement                      | High   | Seed projects from network; free pilot                    |\n| Low student engagement                  | High   | Pre-launch community building; mock projects              |\n| Quality issues in deliverables          | Medium | Structured milestones; admin support; SME review per step |\n| Technical debt from fast development    | Medium | Enforce coding conventions; refactor in V1.1              |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Appendices\n\n### 10.1 MVP Success Criteria (Go/No-Go for V1.1)\n\n| KPI                          | Target                          |\n| :--------------------------- | :------------------------------ |\n| Projects posted              | ≥ 10 in 8-week pilot            |\n| Application rate             | ≥ 3 applicants per project      |\n| Matching success rate        | ≥ 60% of projects matched       |\n| Project completion rate      | ≥ 50% of matched projects       |\n| User willingness to reuse    | ≥ 70%                           |\n| **Pilot scope**              | 15–25 students, 5–8 SMEs, 8 weeks, HCMC |\n\n### 10.2 Feature Evolution Matrix\n\n| Feature                   | MVP              | V1.1 (Q4/2026)       | V2.0 (Q1/2027)       |\n| :------------------------ | :--------------- |       | ✅                    |\n| Escrow Payment            | Simulated        | Real (VNPay/Momo)     | ✅                    |\n| Verified Portfolio & Cert | Basic            | ✅                    |               |\n| Two-way Rating            | —                | ✅                    | ✅                    |\n| Talent Pool               | —                | ✅                    | + Premium [Introduction](#1-introduction)\n2. [Architectural Goals & Principles](#2-architectural-goals--principles)\n3. [High-Level Architecture](#3-high-level-architecture)\n4. [Technology Stack](#4-technology-stack)\n5. [Frontend Architecture](#5-frontend-architecture)\n6. [Backend Architecture](#6-backend-architecture)\n7. [Database Design](#7-database-design)\n8. [Authentication & Authorization](#8-authentication--authorization)\n9. [API Architecture](#9-api-architecture)\n10. [Deployment Architecture](#10-deployment-architecture)\n11. [Security Architecture](#11-security-architecture)\n12. [Scalability & Evolution](#12-scalability--evolution)\n13. [Monitoring & Logging](#13-monitoring--logging)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Introduction\n\nThis document describes **how** the SkillBridge MVP is built. For **what** it must do, see the [SRS](./SRS_MVP.md). For coding conventions, see [Coding Standards](./Source_Code_Documentation.md).\n\n---\n\n## 2. Architectural Goals & Principles\n\n### 2.1 Goals\n\n| Goal                    | Description                                                            |\n| :---------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Rapid Development**   | Deployable within Q3/2026 by a 2-person team                          |\n| **Simplicity**          | Monolithic architecture; avoid over-engineering                        |\n| **Extensibility**       | Design for future modularization (V1.1: escrow, V2.0: AI)             |\n| **Cost Efficiency**     | Free-tier cloud services; minimal operational overhead                 |\n\n### 2.2 Architectural Principles\n\n1. **Modular Monolith** — Well-structured monolith organized by domain modules, enabling future decomposition\n2. **API-First** — Frontend and backend communicate exclusively via REST APIs\n3. **Separation of Concerns** — Clear boundaries between presentation, business logic, and data access\n4. **Security by Design** — Auth, authorization, and input validation built in from the start\n\n---\n\n## 3. High-Level Architecture\n\n### 3.1 System Context\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"External Users\"\n        Student[\"🎓 Student\"]\n        SME[\"🏢 SME\"]\n        Admin[\"⚙️ Admin\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph \"SkillBridge Platform\"\n        WebApp[\"Web Application<br>(Next.js)\"]\n        APIServer[\"API Server<br>(Node.js / Express)\"]\n        DB[\"PostgreSQL\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph \"External Services\"\n        Email[\"Email (SMTP)\"]\n        OAuth[\"Google OAuth\"]\n        Storage[\"Cloud Storage\"]\n    end\n\n    Student -->|HTTPS| WebApp\n    SME -->|HTTPS| WebApp\n    Admin -->|HTTPS| WebApp\n\n    WebApp -->|REST API| APIServer\n    APIServer -->|SQL| DB\n    APIServer -->|SMTP| Email\n    APIServer -->|OAuth 2.0| OAuth\n    APIServer -->|Upload| Storage\n\`\`\`\n\n### 3.2 Component Architecture\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\ngraph LR\n    subgraph \"Frontend (SPA)\"\n        Pages[\"Pages / Routes\"]\n        Components[\"UI Components\"]\n        State[\"State Management\"]\n        APIClient[\"API Client\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph \"Backend (API Server)\"\n        Router[\"Express Router\"]\n        Middleware[\"Middleware\"]\n        Controllers[\"Controllers\"]\n        Services[\"Services\"]\n        DAL[\"Data Access (Prisma)\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph \"Data\"\n        PG[\"PostgreSQL\"]\n    end\n\n    Pages --> Components\n    Pages --> State\n    State --> APIClient\n    APIClient -->|HTTP/JSON| Router\n    Router --> Middleware --> Controllers --> Services --> DAL --> PG\n\`\`\`\n\n---\n\n## 4. Technology Stack\n\n### 4.1 Stack Overview\n\n| Layer              | Technology              | Rationale                                         |\n| :----------------- | :---------------------- | :------------------------------------------------ |\n| **Frontend**       | Next.js (React)         | SSR/SSG, file-based routing, large ecosystem      |\n| **Styling**        | CSS Modules             | Scoped styles, no external deps                   |\n| **Backend**        | Node.js + Express       | Lightweight, full-stack JS synergy                |\n| **Database**       | PostgreSQL              | ACID-compliant, JSON support, free-tier available |\n| **ORM**            | Prisma                  | Type-safe queries, auto-migration                 |\n| **Auth**           | JWT + bcrypt            | Stateless, industry standard                      |\n| **API**            | REST (JSON)             | Simple, sufficient for MVP                        |\n| **Email**          | Nodemailer (SMTP)       | Free with Gmail SMTP                              |\n| **File Storage**   | Cloudinary              | Free-tier image hosting                           |\n| **CI/CD**          | GitHub Actions          | Free, integrated with GitHub                      |\n| **Hosting**        | Vercel (FE) + Railway (BE) | Free-tier, auto-deploy                         |\n\n> [!IMPORTANT]\n> The entire stack uses **TypeScript** (strict mode) for both frontend and backend.\n\n### 4.2 Version Matrix\n\n| Package    | Version  |\n| :--------- | :------- |\n| Node.js    | ≥ 20 LTS |\n| Next.js    | ≥ 14     |\n| React      | ≥ 18     |\n| Express    | ≥ 4.18   |\n| PostgreSQL | ≥ 15     |\n| Prisma     | ≥ 5      |\n| TypeScript | ≥ 5      |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Frontend Architecture\n\n### 5.1 Design Decisions\n\n| Decision               | Approach                           | Example                       |\n| :--------------------- | :--------------------------------- | :---------------------------- |\n| **Routing**            | Next.js App Router (file-based)    | `app/projects/[id]/page.tsx`  |\n| **Components**         | Small, focused, composed into pages| `ProjectCard`, `MilestoneList`|\n| **State (Server)**     | React Query / SWR                  | Project list, applications    |\n| **State (Auth)**       | React Context + localStorage       | Current user, JWT token       |\n| **State (UI)**         | Local `useState`                   | Modal open/close, form inputs |\n| **Forms**              | React Hook Form                    | Project posting, profile edit |\n| **Styling**            | CSS Modules                        | Scoped per component          |\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> Folder structure is defined in [Coding Standards §2](./Source_Code_Documentation.md#2-project-structure).\n\n---\n\n## 6. Backend Architecture\n\n### 6.1 Layered Architecture\n\nEach backend module follows a strict **3-layer pattern**:\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\ngraph TB\n    Request[\"HTTP Request\"] --> Router[\"Router\"]\n    Router --> Middleware[\"Middleware<br>(Auth, Validation)\"]\n    Middleware --> Controller[\"Controller\"]\n    Controller --> Service[\"Service\"]\n    Service --> DAL[\"Prisma ORM\"]\n    DAL --> DB[\"PostgreSQL\"]\n\n    style Controller fill:#4A90D9,color:#fff\n    style Service fill:#7B68EE,color:#fff\n    style DAL fill:#2ECC71,color:#fff\n\`\`\`\n\n| Layer          | Responsibility                                                   |\n| :------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Controller** | Parse request, call service, format response. **No business logic.** |\n| **Service**    | All business logic. **No access to `req`/`res` objects.**         |\n| **Data Access**| Database queries via Prisma. **No business logic.**               |\n\n### 6.2 Middleware Pipeline\n\n| Order | Middleware        | Purpose                                          |\n| :---: | :---------------- | :----------------------------------------------- |\n| 1     | CORS              | Allow frontend origin                            |\n| 2     | Body Parser       | Parse JSON bodies                                |\n| 3     | Request Logger    | Log method, path, status, duration               |\n| 4     | Rate Limiter      | 100 req/min per IP                               |\n| 5     | Auth Middleware    | Verify JWT, attach user                          |\n| 6     | Role Middleware    | Check role permissions                           |\n| 7     | Validation        | Validate body/params via Zod schemas             |\n| 8     | Error Handler     | Catch and format all errors consistently         |\n\n### 6.3 Error Handling Architecture\n\nAll errors flow through a centralized error handler. Custom error classes map to HTTP status codes:\n\n| Error Class        | HTTP | Code                 | Usage                       |\n| :----------------- | :--: | :------------------- | :-------------------------- |\n| `BadRequestError`  | 400  | `BAD_REQUEST`        | Invalid input               |\n| `UnauthorizedError`| 401  | `UNAUTHORIZED`       | Missing/invalid auth        |\n| `ForbiddenError`   | 403  | `FORBIDDEN`          | Insufficient permissions    |\n| `NotFoundError`    | 404  | `NOT_FOUND`          | Resource doesn't exist      |\n| `ConflictError`    | 409  | `CONFLICT`           | Duplicate resource          |\n| `ValidationError`  | 422  | `VALIDATION_ERROR`   | Schema validation failure   |\n\n**Response envelope:**\n\n\`\`\`typescript\n// Success\n{ success: true, data: T, meta?: { total, page, limit, totalPages } }\n\n// Error\n{ success: false, error: { code: string, message: string, details?: unknown } }\n\`\`\`\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> For implementation patterns (controller/service/route code examples), see [Coding Standards §6](./Source_Code_Documentation.md#6-backend-nodejs--express-conventions).\n\n---\n\n## 7. Database Design\n\n### 7.1 Schema\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\nerDiagram\n    users ||--o| student_profiles : has\n    users ||--o| sme_profiles : has\n    sme_profiles ||--o{ projects : posts\n    projects ||--o{ milestones : contains\n    projects ||--o{ applications : receives\n    student_profiles ||--o{ applications : submits\n    student_profiles ||--o{ student_group_members : belongs_to\n    student_groups ||--o{ student_group_members : has\n    student_profiles ||--o{ portfolio_entries : earns\n    student_profiles ||--o{ certificates : receives\n    projects ||--o| portfolio_entries : generates\n    projects ||--o| certificates : generates\n\n    users {\n        uuid id PK\n        string email UK\n        string password_hash\n        enum role\n        boolean is_active\n        datetime created_at\n        datetime updated_at\n    }\n\n    student_profiles {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid user_id FK_UK\n        string full_name\n        string university\n        string major\n        int year\n        text bio\n        json skills\n        string github_url\n        string avatar_url\n    }\n\n    sme_profiles {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid user_id FK_UK\n        string company_name\n        string industry\n        text description\n        string contact_phone\n        string website\n        string logo_url\n    }\n\n    projects {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid sme_id FK\n        string title\n        text description\n        json required_skills\n        decimal budget\n        int duration_weeks\n        enum status\n        enum escrow_status\n        int max_team_size\n        datetime deadline\n        datetime created_at\n        datetime updated_at\n    }\n\n    milestones {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        string title\n        text description\n        int order_index\n        datetime deadline\n        enum status\n        text deliverable_url\n        text feedback\n    }\n\n    applications {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        uuid student_id FK\n        uuid group_id FK\n        text cover_message\n        enum status\n        datetime created_at\n    }\n\n    student_groups {\n        uuid id PK\n        string name\n        uuid leader_id FK\n    }\n\n    student_group_members {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid group_id FK\n        uuid student_id FK\n    }\n\n    portfolio_entries {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid student_id FK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        string project_title\n        text project_description\n        string role_description\n        text deliverables_summary\n        datetime completed_at\n        boolean is_verified\n    }\n\n    certificates {\n        uuid id PK\n        uuid student_id FK\n        uuid project_id FK\n        string student_name\n        string project_title\n        string sme_name\n        datetime issued_at\n        string verification_code UK\n    }\n\`\`\`\n\n### 7.2 Index Strategy\n\n| Table             | Index                      | Type   | Purpose                        |\n| :---------------- | :------------------------- | :----- | :----------------------------- |\n| `users`           | `email`                    | Unique | Login lookup                   |\n| `projects`        | `status`                   | B-Tree | Filter by status               |\n| `projects`        | `sme_id`                   | B-Tree | SME's project list             |\n| `projects`        | `required_skills`          | GIN    | Skill-based matching           |\n| `applications`    | `(project_id, student_id)` | Unique | Prevent duplicates             |\n| `applications`    | `student_id`               | B-Tree | Student's history              |\n| `milestones`      | `project_id`               | B-Tree | Milestone list                 |\n| `portfolio_entries`| `student_id`              | B-Tree | Portfolio listing              |\n| `certificates`    | `verification_code`        | Unique | Certificate verification       |\n\n### 7.3 Migration\n\n- **Prisma Migrate** for schema versioning\n- Development: `npx prisma migrate dev`\n- Production: `npx prisma migrate deploy`\n- Seed data: `npx prisma db seed`\n\n---\n\n## 8. Authentication & Authorization\n\n### 8.1 Authentication Flow\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant User\n    participant Frontend\n    participant API as API Server\n    participant DB\n\n    User->>Frontend: Enter credentials\n    Frontend->>API: POST /api/auth/login\n    API->>DB: Find user by email\n    API->>API: Verify password (bcrypt)\n    API-->>Frontend: { token: JWT, user: {...} }\n    Frontend->>Frontend: Store token in localStorage\n\n    Note over Frontend,API: Subsequent requests include Bearer token\n\n    Frontend->>API: GET /api/projects\n    API->>API: Verify JWT, extract user\n    API-->>Frontend: Response\n\`\`\`\n\n### 8.2 JWT Payload\n\n\`\`\`json\n{\n  \"sub\": \"user-uuid\",\n  \"email\": \"user@example.com\",\n  \"role\": \"STUDENT | SME | ADMIN\",\n  \"iat\": 1719792000,\n  \"exp\": 1719878400\n}\n\`\`\`\n\n### 8.3 RBAC Matrix\n\n| Action              | Student | SME | Admin |\n| :------------------ | :-----: | :-: | :---: |\n| View Projects       | ✅      | ✅  | ✅    |\n| Create Project      | ❌      | ✅  | ✅    |\n| Apply to Project    | ✅      | ❌  | ❌    |\n| Review Applications | ❌      | ✅  | ✅    |\n| Confirm Match       | ❌      | ✅  | ✅    |\n| Submit Milestone    | ✅      | ❌  | ❌    |\n| Approve Milestone   | ❌      | ✅  | ✅    |\n| Accept Project      | ❌      | ✅  | ✅    |\n| View Portfolio      | ✅      | ✅  | ✅    |\n| Manage Users        | ❌      | ❌  | ✅    |\n| Moderate Content    | ❌      | ❌  | ✅    |\n\n---\n\n## 9. API Architecture\n\n### 9.1 Conventions\n\n| Convention       | Standard                                                            |\n| :--------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------ |\n| **Base URL**     | `/api/v1`                                                           |\n| **Methods**      | `GET` = read, `POST` = create, `PUT` = update, `DELETE` = delete    |\n| **Naming**       | Plural nouns: `/projects`, `/applications`                          |\n| **Response**     | JSON with `{ success, data, meta? }` or `{ success, error }` envelope |\n| **Pagination**   | `?page=1&limit=10` → `meta: { total, page, limit, totalPages }`    |\n| **Filtering**    | Query params: `?status=PUBLISHED&skills=react,nodejs`               |\n| **Sorting**      | `?sort=created_at&order=desc`                                       |\n| **Status Codes** | 200, 201, 400, 401, 403, 404, 422, 500                             |\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> Full endpoint catalog is in [SRS §7](./SRS_MVP.md#7-api-specification).\n\n---\n\n## 10. Deployment Architecture\n\n### 10.1 Pipeline\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\ngraph TB\n    Dev[\"Local Dev\"] -->|git push| Repo[\"GitHub\"]\n    Repo -->|trigger| Actions[\"GitHub Actions (CI/CD)\"]\n    Actions -->|deploy FE| Vercel[\"Vercel (Frontend)\"]\n    Actions -->|deploy BE| Railway[\"Railway (Backend + PostgreSQL)\"]\n\`\`\`\n\n### 10.2 Environments\n\n| Environment     | Frontend               | Backend                    | Database              |\n| :-------------- | :--------------------- | :------------------------- | :-------------------- |\n| **Development** | localhost:3000          | localhost:4000             | localhost:5432        |\n| **Staging**     | staging.skillbridge.vn | api-staging.skillbridge.vn | Railway (staging)     |\n| **Production**  | skillbridge.vn         | api.skillbridge.vn         | Railway (production)  |\n\n### 10.3 Environment Variables\n\n\`\`\`bash\n# Backend\nNODE_ENV=production\nPORT=4000\nDATABASE_URL=postgresql://user:pass@host:5432/skillbridge\nJWT_SECRET=<random-64-char>\nJWT_EXPIRES_IN=24h\nCORS_ORIGIN=https://skillbridge.vn\nSMTP_HOST=smtp.gmail.com\nSMTP_PORT=587\nSMTP_USER=noreply@skillbridge.vn\nSMTP_PASS=<app-password>\nCLOUDINARY_URL=cloudinary://key:secret@cloud\n\n# Frontend\nNEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=https://api.skillbridge.vn/api/v1\nNEXT_PUBLIC_APP_NAME=SkillBridge\n\`\`\`\n\n---\n\n## 11. Security Architecture\n\n### 11.1 Threat Mitigations\n\n| Threat                       | Mitigation                                             |\n| :--------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------- |\n| **SQL Injection**            | Prisma ORM (parameterized queries); no raw SQL         |\n| **XSS**                     | React auto-escaping; helmet.js CSP headers             |\n| **CSRF**                    | Token-based auth (JWT), no session cookies              |\n| **Brute Force Login**       | Rate limiting on auth endpoints (5 attempts/min)        |\n| **Data Exposure**           | Role-based API access; query scoping by ownership       |\n| **Sensitive Data**          | bcrypt hashing; JWT secret rotation                     |\n| **HTTPS**                   | Enforced via Vercel/Railway (automatic SSL)             |\n| **Dependency Vulns**        | `npm audit` in CI; Dependabot alerts                    |\n\n---\n\n## 12. Scalability & Evolution\n\n### 12.1 Optimization Strategies (MVP)\n\n| Strategy               | Application                                         |\n| :--------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------- |\n| Server-Side Rendering  | Next.js SSR for initial loads (SEO + perf)          |\n| Static Generation      | Landing page, public portfolios                     |\n| Database Indexing      | Key fields indexed (see §7.2)                       |\n| Query Optimization     | Prisma `select`/`include` to avoid N+1              |\n| Image Optimization     | Next.js `<Image>` + Cloudinary CDN                  |\n\n### 12.2 Scalability Path\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\ngraph LR\n    subgraph \"MVP\"\n        A[\"Modular Monolith\"]\n    end\n    subgraph \"V1.1\"\n        B[\"+ Redis Cache<br>+ BullMQ\"]\n    end\n    subgraph \"V2.0\"\n        C[\"API Gateway\"]\n        D[\"Auth Service\"]\n        E[\"Project Service\"]\n        F[\"AI Matching\"]\n        G[\"Notifications\"]\n    end\n    A -->|Growth| B -->|Scale| C\n    C --> D\n    C --> E\n    C --> F\n    C --> G\n\`\`\`\n\n### 12.3 Key Technical Decisions\n\n| Decision                   | MVP Choice       | Future Consideration                                |\n| :------------------------- | :--------------- | :-------------------------------------------------- |\n| Monolith vs. Microservices | Monolith         | Decompose when team > 5 or traffic > 10k MAU        |\n| SQL vs. NoSQL              | PostgreSQL       | Add NoSQL for unstructured data if needed            |\n| REST vs. GraphQL           | REST             | Consider GraphQL for mobile app (V2.0)              |\n| Payment Gateway            | Simulated        | VNPay / Momo in V1.1                                |\n| Search Engine              | PostgreSQL FTS   | Meilisearch in V2.0                                 |\n| Message Queue              | None             | BullMQ (Redis) in V1.1                              |\n\n---\n\n## 13. Monitoring & Logging\n\n### 13.1 Logging\n\n| Level   | Usage                                         |\n| :------ | :-------------------------------------------- |\n| `error` | Unhandled exceptions, critical failures       |\n| `warn`  | Unexpected but recoverable situations         |\n| `info`  | Business events (user registered, project posted) |\n| `debug` | Technical detail (dev/staging only)           |\n\n**Format**: Structured JSON with `timestamp`, `level`, `message`, `context`, `requestId`.\n\n### 13.2 Monitoring Tools\n\n| Tool            | Purpose                            | Tier |\n| :-------------- | :--------------------------------- | :--- |\n| Railway Metrics | Server CPU, memory, requests       | Free |\n| Vercel Analytics| Frontend perf & Core Web Vitals    | Free |\n| Sentry          | Error tracking & alerting          | Free |\n| UptimeRobot     | Uptime monitoring                  | Free |\n\n---\n\n*End of System Architecture Document — SkillBridge MVP v2.0*\n", "Description": "Revised SAD: removed duplicated folder structures (now in Coding Standards), API endpoint catalog (moved to SRS), performance targets (in SRS NFRs), evolution matrix (in SRS appendix), security headers listing, error handling code examples. [Documentation Standards](#11-documentation-standards)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Overview\n\nThis document defines conventions that **cannot be enforced by automated tools**. Formatting rules (semicolons, quotes, indentation, trailing commas, import ordering, `const` vs `let`, etc.) are handled by **ESLint + Prettier** and are not repeated here.\n\n**Automated enforcement stack:**\n\n| Tool           | Purpose                                |\n| :------------- | :------------------------------------- |\n| **ESLint**     | Static analysis & code rules           |\n| **Prettier**   | Code formatting                        |\n| **TypeScript** | Type checking (strict mode)            |\n| **Husky**      | Pre-commit hooks (lint + format)       |\n| **lint-staged**| Run linters on staged files only       |\n\nSee config files in the repository root: `.eslintrc.js`, `.prettierrc`, `tsconfig.json`.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Project Structure\n\n### 2.1 Repository Layout\n\n\`\`\`\nskillbridge/\n├── frontend/               # Next.js application\n├── backend/                # Express API server\n├── docs/                   # Documentation\n├── .github/workflows/      # CI/CD pipelines\n├── .gitignore\n├── README.md\n└── package.json            # Root workspace config\n\`\`\`\n\n### 2.2 Frontend Structure\n\n\`\`\`\nfrontend/src/\n├── app/                    # Next.js App Router\n│   ├── (auth)/             # Auth pages (login, register)\n│   ├── (dashboard)/        # Dashboard pages (student, sme, admin)\n│   ├── projects/           # Project listing & [id] detail\n│   ├── profile/[id]/       # Profile pages\n│   ├── layout.tsx          # Root layout\n│   ├── page.tsx            # Home page\n│   ├── loading.tsx         # Global loading\n│   ├── error.tsx           # Error boundary\n│   └── not-found.tsx       # 404\n├── components/             # Reusable UI components\n│   ├── common/             # Button, Input, Modal, Card, Badge, Spinner\n│   ├── layout/             # Header, Footer, Sidebar\n│   ├── project/            # ProjectCard, ProjectList, ProjectForm\n│   ├── profile/            # StudentProfile, SMEProfile, SkillBadge\n│   └── milestone/          # MilestoneList, MilestoneCard\n├── hooks/                  # useAuth, useProjects, useApplications, useMilestones\n├── lib/                    # api.ts, auth.ts, utils.ts, constants.ts\n├── types/                  # user.ts, project.ts, application.ts, milestone.ts, api.ts\n├── styles/                 # globals.css, variables.css\n└── context/                # AuthContext, ThemeContext\n\`\`\`\n\n### 2.3 Backend Structure\n\n\`\`\`\nbackend/src/\n├── config/                 # database.ts, env.ts, cors.ts\n├── modules/                # Feature modules (domain-driven)\n│   ├── auth/               # auth.controller.ts, auth.service.ts, auth.routes.ts,\n│   │                       # auth.validation.ts, auth.types.ts\n│   ├── user/               # (same pattern)\n│   ├── project/\n│   ├── application/\n│   ├── milestone/\n│   ├── matching/\n│   ├── escrow/\n│   ├── portfolio/\n│   └── certificate/\n├── middleware/             # auth, role, validation, rateLimiter, error\n├── utils/                  # logger.ts, response.ts, errors.ts, pagination.ts\n├── prisma/                 # schema.prisma, seed.ts, migrations/\n├── app.ts                  # Express app setup\n└── server.ts               # Entry point\n\`\`\`\n\n### 2.4 File Organization Rules\n\n| Rule                                      | Rationale                              |\n| :---------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------- |\n| One component/class per file              | Easy to locate and maintain            |\n| File name matches the default export      | `ProjectCard.tsx` exports `ProjectCard` |\n| Group by feature/domain, not by file type | `modules/project/` not `controllers/`  |\n| Use barrel exports (`index.ts`)           | Clean imports: `from '@/components/common'` |\n| Keep files under 300 lines                | Refactor if longer                     |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Naming Conventions\n\n### 3.1 Reference Table\n\n| Element                   | Convention          | Example                                      |\n| :------------------------ | :------------------ | :------------------------------------------- |\n| **Files (Component)**     | PascalCase          | `ProjectCard.tsx`                            |\n| **Files (Non-component)** | camelCase           | `useProjects.ts`, `auth.service.ts`          |\n| **Files (Config/Style)**  | kebab-case          | `globals.css`, `next.config.js`              |\n| **Directories**           | kebab-case          | `common/`, `milestone/`                      |\n| **Variables**             | camelCase           | `projectList`, `isLoading`                   |\n| **Constants**             | UPPER_SNAKE_CASE    | `MAX_TEAM_SIZE`, `JWT_EXPIRES_IN`            |\n| **Functions**             | camelCase (verb-first) | `getProjectById()`, `validateInput()`     |\n| **Classes**               | PascalCase          | `ProjectService`, `AppError`                 |\n| **Interfaces/Types**      | PascalCase          | `CreateProjectDTO`, `ApiResponse<T>`         |\n| **Enums**                 | PascalCase          | `ProjectStatus`                              |\n| **Enum Members**          | UPPER_SNAKE_CASE    | `ProjectStatus.IN_PROGRESS`                  |\n| **React Components**      | PascalCase          | `<ProjectCard />`                            |\n| **Custom Hooks**          | `use` prefix        | `useAuth()`, `useProjects()`                 |\n| **CSS Classes (modules)** | camelCase           | `.projectCard`, `.milestoneTitle`            |\n| **Database Tables**       | snake_case (plural) | `users`, `student_profiles`                  |\n| **Database Columns**      | snake_case          | `created_at`, `user_id`                      |\n| **API Endpoints**         | kebab-case (plural) | `/api/v1/projects`                           |\n| **Environment Variables** | UPPER_SNAKE_CASE    | `DATABASE_URL`, `JWT_SECRET`                 |\n\n### 3.2 Key Rules\n\n- **Booleans**: Use `is`, `has`, `can`, `should` prefixes (`isAuthenticated`, `hasApplied`)\n- **Functions**: Verb-first (`getProjectById`, `createApplication`, `validateInput`)\n- **Handlers**: `handle` prefix (`handleSubmit`, `handleProjectClick`)\n\n---\n\n## 4. TypeScript Standards\n\n### 4.1 Type Safety Rules\n\n| Rule                                   | Enforcement  |\n| :------------------------------------- | :----------: |\n| Never use `any`                        | ❌ Forbidden |\n| Use `unknown` for truly unknown types  | ✅ Preferred |\n| Define interfaces for all API responses| ✅ Required  |\n| Define DTOs for all request bodies     | ✅ Required  |\n| Use `enum` or union types for fixed sets| ✅ Required |\n| Minimize type assertions (`as`)        | ⚠️ Minimize |\n\n### 4.2 Reference Configuration\n\n\`\`\`jsonc\n// tsconfig.json — key options\n{\n  \"compilerOptions\": {\n    \"strict\": true,\n    \"noImplicitAny\": true,\n    \"strictNullChecks\": true,\n    \"noUnusedLocals\": true,\n    \"noUnusedParameters\": true,\n    \"noImplicitReturns\": true\n  }\n}\n\`\`\`\n\n---\n\n## 5. React & Next.js Conventions\n\n### 5.1 Component Structure\n\nEvery component SHOULD follow this internal order:\n\n1. **Imports** (external → internal → relative → styles)\n2. **Type definitions** (props interface)\n3. **Component function** (named export preferred)\n   - a. Hooks\n   - b. Derived state\n   - c. Event handlers\n   - d. Effects\n   - e. Early returns (loading, error)\n   - f. Render\n\n### 5.2 Component Rules\n\n| Rule                                              | Rationale                         |\n| :------------------------------------------------ | :-------------------------------- |\n| One component per file                            | Clarity                           |\n| Functional components only (no class components)  | Modern React                      |\n| Props defined with TypeScript interfaces          | Type safety                       |\n| Named exports (not default exports)               | Better refactoring                |\n| Components under 150 lines                        | Extract sub-components if longer  |\n| Complex logic extracted into custom hooks         | Separation of concerns            |\n| CSS Modules for styling                           | Scoped, no collisions             |\n\n---\n\n## 6. Backend Conventions\n\n### 6.1 Module File Pattern\n\nEach backend module follows this structure:\n\n| File                 | Responsibility                                         |\n| :------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------- |\n| `*.routes.ts`        | Express routes, attach middleware and controller        |\n| `*.controller.ts`    | Parse request, call service, format response            |\n| `*.service.ts`       | Business logic, database access via Prisma              |\n| `*.validation.ts`    | Zod schemas for request validation                      |\n| `*.types.ts`         | TypeScript interfaces for the module                    |\n\n### 6.2 Critical Layer Rules\n\n> [!IMPORTANT]\n> These rules ensure testability and maintainability:\n> - **Controllers** MUST NOT contain business logic or access the database\n> - **Controllers** MUST delegate all logic to the service layer\n> - **Controllers** MUST use `next(error)` for error propagation\n> - **Services** MUST NOT access `req` or `res` objects\n> - **Services** throw custom `AppError` subclasses for error conditions\n\n---\n\n## 7. SOLID Principles\n\n### 7.1 Application to SkillBridge\n\n| Principle | Application                                                                |\n| :-------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **SRP**   | Controller / Service / Validation separation in every module                |\n| **OCP**   | Strategy pattern for matching (MVP: skill-tag → V1.1: rule-based → V2.0: AI) |\n| **LSP**   | Escrow providers are interchangeable (Simulated → VNPay)                    |\n| **ISP**   | Small interfaces: `ProjectReader`, `ProjectWriter`, `ProjectStatusManager`  |\n| **DIP**   | Services depend on interfaces (`NotificationSender`, `EscrowProvider`)      |\n\n### 7.2 Example: Open/Closed Principle (Matching Strategy)\n\n\`\`\`typescript\n// Interface — stable, never modified\nexport interface MatchingStrategy {\n  findMatches(project: Project, candidates: Student[]): ScoredStudent[];\n}\n\n// MVP implementation\nexport class SkillTagMatchingStrategy implements MatchingStrategy {\n  findMatches(project: Project, candidates: Student[]): ScoredStudent[] {\n    return candidates\n      .map(s => ({ student: s, score: this.calculateOverlap(project.requiredSkills, s.skills) }))\n      .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);\n  }\n}\n\n// V1.1: Add without modifying existing code\nexport class RuleBasedMatchingStrategy implements MatchingStrategy { /* ... */ }\n\n// V2.0: Add without modifying existing code\nexport class AIMatchingStrategy implements MatchingStrategy { /* ... */ }\n\n// Service uses dependency injection\nexport class MatchingService {\n  constructor(private strategy: MatchingStrategy) {}\n  async getRecommendations(projectId: string) {\n    const project = await this.getProject(projectId);\n    const candidates = await this.getCandidates(project);\n    return this.strategy.findMatches(project, candidates);\n  }\n}\n\`\`\`\n\n---\n\n## 8. Testing Standards\n\n### 8.1 Strategy\n\n| Test Type          | Scope                            | Tool             | Target         |\n| :----------------- | :------------------------------- | :--------------- | :------------- |\n| **Unit Tests**     | Services, utilities, validation  | Jest             | ≥ 60% (core)   |\n| **Integration**    | API endpoints (route → DB)       | Jest + Supertest | Key flows      |\n| **E2E**            | Critical user flows              | Playwright       | Deferred to V1.1|\n\n### 8.2 Conventions\n\n| Convention                                   | Example                                    |\n| :------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------- |\n| Unit test: `*.test.ts`                       | `project.service.test.ts`                  |\n| Integration: `*.integration.test.ts`         | `project.routes.integration.test.ts`       |\n| Test utility: `*.helper.ts`                  | `auth.helper.ts`                           |\n| Use AAA pattern (Arrange, Act, Assert)       | —                                          |\n| Descriptive names: `should...when...`        | `should create project with valid data`    |\n| Mock external dependencies                   | DB, email service                          |\n| Test behavior, not implementation details    | —                                          |\n| Clean up test data in `afterEach`/`afterAll` | —                                          |\n\n---\n\n## 9. Git Workflow\n\n### 9.1 Branch Strategy\n\n\`\`\`mermaid\ngitgraph\n    commit id: \"Initial setup\"\n    branch develop\n    checkout develop\n    commit id: \"feat: auth module\"\n    branch feature/milestone-tracking\n    checkout feature/milestone-tracking\n    commit id: \"feat: milestone CRUD\"\n    checkout develop\n    merge feature/milestone-tracking id: \"Merge\"\n    checkout main\n    merge develop id: \"Release v0.1.0\"\n\`\`\`\n\n| Branch       | Purpose                    | Source    | Merges Into        |\n| :----------- | :------------------------- | :------- | :----------------- |\n| `main`       | Production-ready code      | —        | —                  |\n| `develop`    | Integration branch         | `main`   | `main`             |\n| `feature/*`  | New features               | `develop`| `develop`          |\n| `bugfix/*`   | Bug fixes                  | `develop`| `develop`          |\n| `hotfix/*`   | Critical production fixes  | `main`   | `main` + `develop` |\n\n### 9.2 Commit Convention\n\nFollow [Conventional Commits](https://www.conventionalcommits.org/):\n\n\`\`\`\n<type>(<scope>): <description>\n\`\`\`\n\n| Type       | Usage                                | Example                                |\n| :--------- | :----------------------------------- | :------------------------------------- |\n| `feat`     | New feature                          | `feat(project): add milestone tracking`|\n| `fix`      | Bug fix                              | `fix(auth): resolve JWT expiration`    |\n| `docs`     | Documentation                        | `docs: update SRS`                     |\n| `refactor` | Code change (no fix/feat)            | `refactor(matching): extract strategy` |\n| `test`     | Adding/updating tests                | `test(project): add unit tests`        |\n| `chore`    | Maintenance, dependencies            | `chore: update Prisma to v5.10`        |\n\n### 9.3 Pull Request Rules\n\n| Rule                                         | Requirement |\n| :------------------------------------------- | :---------: |\n| Title follows commit convention              | Required    |\n| Description explains **what** and **why**    | Required    |\n| All CI checks pass                           | Required    |\n| At least 1 code review approval              | Required    |\n| No direct commits to `main` or `develop`     | Required    |\n| PR size ≤ 400 lines changed                  | Recommended |\n| Linked to issue/task                         | Recommended |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Code Review Checklist\n\nWhen reviewing a Pull Request:\n\n**Correctness**\n- [ ] Does the code do what the PR says?\n- [ ] Are edge cases handled?\n- [ ] Are error conditions handled?\n\n**Design**\n- [ ] Follows layered architecture (Controller → Service → DAL)?\n- [ ] Respects SOLID principles?\n- [ ] No unnecessary complexity?\n- [ ] No hard-coded values (should be constants/config)?\n\n**Code Quality**\n- [ ] Follows naming conventions?\n- [ ] Readable without excessive comments?\n- [ ] Functions/components small and focused?\n- [ ] TypeScript types used properly (no `any`)?\n\n**Security**\n- [ ] Inputs validated?\n- [ ] Authorization checked (correct role)?\n- [ ] Sensitive data handled properly?\n- [ ] No SQL injection or XSS risk?\n\n**Testing**\n- [ ] Tests for new business logic?\n- [ ] Existing tests still pass?\n- [ ] Test names descriptive?\n\n---\n\n## 11. Documentation Standards\n\n### 11.1 Code Comments\n\n- **Comment WHY, not WHAT**\n- Write comments for non-obvious decisions, workarounds, and business context\n- Don't comment obvious code\n\n\`\`\`typescript\n// ✅ GOOD — Explains WHY\n// Simulated escrow in MVP; real payment gateway requires Legal approval (V1.1)\nasync function depositEscrow(projectId: string) { /* ... */ }\n\n// ❌ BAD — States the obvious\n// This function deposits escrow\nasync function depositEscrow(projectId: string) { /* ... */ }\n\`\`\`\n\n### 11.2 JSDoc\n\nUse JSDoc for **public service methods**. Include `@param`, `@returns`, `@throws`.\n\n### 11.3 Module README\n\nEach backend module SHOULD have a brief README with: purpose, file list, key concepts.\n\n---\n\n*End of Coding Standards — SkillBridge MVP v2.0*\n", "Description": "Revised Coding Standards: removed all ESLint/Prettier-enforceable rules (const/let, arrow functions, template literals, destructuring, import ordering, etc.), removed duplicate content from SAD (error handling code, API conventions, database/ORM conventions), removed full config file contents (belong in repo), kept only manually-enforced conventions: naming, component/service patterns, SOLID with one concrete example, Git workflow, code review checklist.", "Overwrite": true, "TargetFile": "c:\\Users\\Public\\Projects\\SkillBridge\\docs\\Source_Code_Documentation.md", "toolAction": "Rewriting coding standards", "toolSummary": "Revised coding standards"}

<USER_REQUEST>
Please revise the SRS document based on the following review comments. The goal is to improve requirement clarity, eliminate ambiguous business logic, and ensure the SRS is fully aligned with the MVP scope defined in the latest Business Plan.

General requirements:
- Preserve the current document structure as much as possible.
- Do not introduce any features outside the MVP scope.
- Keep the document concise and implementation-oriented.
- Update related Use Cases, Functional Requirements, Business Rules, API Design and Data Model if affected by the changes below.

====================================================================
1. Matching Workflow
====================================================================

The current "Semi-automatic Matching" description is too vague.

Please explicitly define the workflow:

1. SME creates a project.
2. Students submit applications.
3. System recommends suitable applicants based on predefined skill tags (simple rule-based filtering only, NOT AI).
4. SME reviews the applicant list.
5. SME selects individual students to join the project.
6. Admin may manually assist only when necessary.

Clarifications:
- This is NOT AI Matching.
- Recommendation is based only on predefined project tags and student skill tags.
- AI Matching belongs to a future version, not MVP.

====================================================================
2. Group Apply
====================================================================

Simplify the group application mechanism.

Instead of introducing Team entities or Team Application:

- Every student submits an individual application.
- SME may select multiple applicants for one project.
- A project can therefore have multiple accepted applicants (2–4 students).
- No Team table.
- No Team invitation.
- No Team leader.
- No Team management features.

Update all related use cases and business rules accordingly.

====================================================================

====================================================================

Draft
↓

↓

Matched
↓

In Progress
↓

Pending Acceptance
↓

Completed

↓

↓

↓

↓

Withdrawn

or

====================================================================

↓

↓

1.
Accept
→ Project Completed

Request Revision
→ Milestone becomes Revision Required
→ Student updates work
→ Student resubmits

3.
No response

Day 7
- System records Reminder #1 (business rule only)
- No email or notification implementation in MVP

Day 14
- System records Reminder #2 (business rule only)

- No email or notification implementation in MVP

Day 28

Completed (Auto Accepted)

Do NOT implement email sending.

Only document these as business rules and automatic scheduled actions.

No real payment gateway.

Pending

Each verified project should contain:

- Project title

This is an internal SkillBridge portfolio.

Not a CV.
Not a Resume.

- Admin

Clearly specify permissions for each major function.

Examples:
